  const PAL = {

  const DUNGEON_GUARDS = ['wisp', 'rockspider', 'shadow', 'skullking', 'blackgolem', 'minotaur', 'dragonet', 'darkknight', 'lich'];
  function dungeonGuardFor(depth) {
    return depth >= 29 ? 'boss' : DUNGEON_GUARDS[Math.min(DUNGEON_GUARDS.length - 1, Math.floor((Math.max(1, depth) - 1) / 3))];
  }
  // 地下の番人が守る深さ（ずかんの再戦で使う）
  function dungeonDepthsOf(id) {
    if (id === 'boss') return [29, 30];
    const i = DUNGEON_GUARDS.indexOf(id);
    if (i < 0) return [];
    const out = [];
    for (let d = 1; d <= 28; d += 1) if (dungeonGuardFor(d) === id) out.push(d);
    return out;
  }

  function guardFor(floor) { return FLOOR_GUARD[floor] || 'stone'; }

  function byId(id) {
    for (let i = 0; i < SPRITES.length; i += 1) if (SPRITES[i].id === id) return SPRITES[i];
    return null;
  }

  // ドット絵：外周に1pxの黒いふちを自動でつける
  function pixels(rows, pal) {
    const out = [];
    const w = rows[0].length, h = rows.length;
    const has = function (x, y) { return y >= 0 && y < h && x >= 0 && x < w && !!pal[rows[y][x]]; };
    for (let y = -1; y <= h; y += 1) {
      for (let x = -1; x <= w; x += 1) {
        if (has(x, y)) out.push([x + 1, y + 1, pal[rows[y][x]]]);
        else if (has(x - 1, y) || has(x + 1, y) || has(x, y - 1) || has(x, y + 1)) out.push([x + 1, y + 1, '#1e1d2a']);
      }
    }
    return { out: out, w: w + 2, h: h + 2 };
  }

  // SVG で描く（shape-rendering: crispEdges）。同じ色が横に続くところは1本の rect にまとめる
  function sprite(rows, pal, scale) {
    const s = scale || 1;
    const px = pixels(rows, pal);
    const svg = document.createElementNS(NS, 'svg');
    svg.setAttribute('width', px.w * s);
    svg.setAttribute('height', px.h * s);
    svg.setAttribute('viewBox', '0 0 ' + px.w + ' ' + px.h);
    svg.setAttribute('shape-rendering', 'crispEdges');
    svg.setAttribute('class', 'sprite');
    svg.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');
    const map = {};
    px.out.forEach(function (p) { map[p[0] + ',' + p[1]] = p[2]; });
    for (let y = 0; y < px.h; y += 1) {
      let x = 0;
      while (x < px.w) {
        const c = map[x + ',' + y];
        if (!c) { x += 1; continue; }
        let run = 1;
        while (map[(x + run) + ',' + y] === c) run += 1;
        const r = document.createElementNS(NS, 'rect');
        r.setAttribute('x', x); r.setAttribute('y', y); r.setAttribute('width', run); r.setAttribute('height', 1); r.setAttribute('fill', c);
        svg.appendChild(r);
        x += run;
      }
    }
    return svg;
  }

  // 決まった箱（w×h px）に収まる いちばん大きい倍率で描く（足元をそろえる）
  function fitSprite(id, maxW, maxH) {
    const sp = byId(id);
    if (!sp) return null;
    const s = Math.min(maxH / (sp.rows.length + 2), maxW / (sp.rows[0].length + 2));
    return sprite(sp.rows, PAL, s);
  }

  function icon(name, scale) {
    const ic = ICONS[name];
    return ic ? sprite(ic.rows, ic.pal, scale || 3) : null;
  }

  // キャンバスに描く（背景の絵の中の人物など）
  function drawOnCanvas(ctx, rows, pal, ox, oy, scale) {
    const s = scale || 1;
    pixels(rows, pal).out.forEach(function (p) {
      ctx.fillStyle = p[2];
      ctx.fillRect(ox + p[0] * s, oy + p[1] * s, s, s);
    });
  }

  root.MT = root.MT || {};
  root.MT.Sprites = {
    PAL: PAL, SPRITES: SPRITES, FLOOR_GUARD: FLOOR_GUARD, DUNGEON_GUARDS: DUNGEON_GUARDS,
    guardFor: guardFor, dungeonGuardFor: dungeonGuardFor, dungeonDepthsOf: dungeonDepthsOf, byId: byId,
    HERO_PAL: HERO_PAL, HERO_FRONT: HERO_FRONT, HERO_BACK: HERO_BACK, ICONS: ICONS,
    pixels: pixels, sprite: sprite, fitSprite: fitSprite, icon: icon, drawOnCanvas: drawOnCanvas
  };
})(typeof globalThis !== 'undefined' ? globalThis : this);


/* 面積の塔: 背景の絵（384×288 のキャンバスに 1px 単位で描く）
 * デザインの各画面の draw() をそのまま移植した。image-rendering: pixelated で拡大して使う。
 */
(function (root) {
  'use strict';
  const W = 384, H = 288;

  function makeRnd(seed0) {
    let seed = seed0;
    return function () { seed |= 0; seed = seed + 0x6D2B79F5 | 0; let t = Math.imul(seed ^ seed >>> 15, 1 | seed); t = t + Math.imul(t ^ t >>> 7, 61 | t) ^ t; return ((t ^ t >>> 14) >>> 0) / 4294967296; };
  }
  // 1px ずつの点を、同じ色・同じ行で となりあう（または 1つおきの）ものを まとめて 1回で ぬる。
  // 絵は 1px ずつ ぬったときと まったく同じ（点どうしは 重ならないので、うすい色を かさねても 同じ）。
  // 地下の絵は 点が 数十万あり、そのままだと Chromebook で 数秒 止まるため。
  function batcher(ctx) {
    let run = null;            // { x0, y, n, step(1 か 2), c, a }
    let curStyle = null;
    const patterns = {};
    function style(s) { if (curStyle !== s) { ctx.fillStyle = s; curStyle = s; } }
    function pattern(c, parity) {
      const k = c + '|' + parity;
      if (!patterns[k]) {
        const cv = document.createElement('canvas');
        cv.width = 2; cv.height = 2;
        const g = cv.getContext('2d');
        g.fillStyle = c;
        if (parity === 0) { g.fillRect(0, 0, 1, 1); g.fillRect(1, 1, 1, 1); } else { g.fillRect(1, 0, 1, 1); g.fillRect(0, 1, 1, 1); }
        patterns[k] = ctx.createPattern(cv, 'repeat');
      }
      return patterns[k];
    }
    function flush() {
      if (!run) return;
      const r = run;
      run = null;
      const now = ctx.globalAlpha;
      if (now !== r.a) ctx.globalAlpha = r.a;
      if (r.step === 2 && r.n > 1) {
        // 絵の外（x<0）から はじまる列は、絵の中の 最初の点から ぬる（外の点は どのみち 見えない）
        let x0 = r.x0, n = r.n;
        if (x0 < 0) { const k = Math.ceil(-x0 / 2); x0 += 2 * k; n -= k; }
        if (x0 + 2 * (n - 1) >= W) n = Math.floor((W - 1 - x0) / 2) + 1;
        if (n > 0 && r.y >= 0 && r.y < H) { ctx.fillStyle = pattern(r.c, (x0 + r.y) & 1); curStyle = null; ctx.fillRect(x0, r.y, 2 * n - 1, 1); }
      }
      else { style(r.c); ctx.fillRect(r.x0, r.y, r.step === 1 ? r.n : 1, 1); }
      if (now !== r.a) ctx.globalAlpha = now;
    }
    function px(x, y, c) {
      const a = ctx.globalAlpha;
      if (x !== (x | 0) || y !== (y | 0)) { flush(); style(c); ctx.fillRect(x, y, 1, 1); return; }
      if (run && run.y === y && run.c === c && run.a === a) {
        if (run.n === 1 && (x === run.x0 + 1 || x === run.x0 + 2)) { run.step = x - run.x0; run.n = 2; return; }
        if (run.n > 1 && x === run.x0 + run.n * run.step) { run.n += 1; return; }
      }
      flush();
      run = { x0: x, y: y, n: 1, step: 1, c: c, a: a };
    }
    function rect(x, y, w, h, c) { flush(); style(c); ctx.fillRect(x, y, w, h); }
    return { px: px, rect: rect, flush: flush };
  }
  function tools(ctx) {
    if (!ctx.__batch) ctx.__batch = batcher(ctx);
    return { px: ctx.__batch.px, rect: ctx.__batch.rect };
  }
  function pixelsOf(rows, pal) { return root.MT.Sprites.pixels(rows, pal); }

  // 塔の中の石壁（問題・解説・やり直し・結果で共通）
  function stoneWall(px, rect, bottom) {
    rect(0, 0, W, H, '#5e574f');
    for (let yy = 0; yy < bottom; yy++) {
      const row = yy >> 2; if (yy % 4 === 3) { rect(0, yy, W, 1, '#3f3934'); continue; }
      const off = (row & 1) * 5;
      for (let xx = 0; xx < W; xx++) {
        if (((xx + off) % 10) === 0) { px(xx, yy, '#3f3934'); continue; }
        const v = (Math.imul(((xx + off) / 10 | 0) * 131 + row * 17, 2654435761) >>> 0) % 9;
        if (v === 0) px(xx, yy, '#6d655c'); else if (v === 1) px(xx, yy, '#524b44'); if (yy % 4 === 0) px(xx, yy, '#6d655c');
      }
    }
  }
  function stoneFloor(px, rect, rnd, top, dots) {
    rect(0, top, W, H - top, '#4a4a58'); rect(0, top, W, 2, '#2b2a3a');
    for (let y = top + 2; y < H; y++) { const step = 4 + ((y - top - 2) / 14 | 0) * 2; if (((y - top - 2) % step) === 0) rect(0, y, W, 1, '#3a3a48'); }
    for (let i = 0; i < dots; i++) px(rnd() * W | 0, top + 2 + (rnd() * (H - top - 2) | 0), '#55556a');
  }
  function torchSmall(ctx, px, rect, x, y, reach) {
    rect(x - 1, y + 3, 3, 7, '#5a3a22'); rect(x - 2, y, 5, 3, '#f4a23c'); rect(x - 1, y - 2, 3, 2, '#ffd37c'); px(x, y - 3, '#ffe7a3');
    ctx.globalAlpha = 0.22;
    for (let d = 0; d < reach; d++) for (let dx = -d; dx <= d; dx++) { px(x + dx, y - reach + d, '#ffb347'); px(x + dx, y + reach - d, '#ffb347'); }
    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
  }

  // ---- 問題（窓・たいまつ・床のタイル）----
  function room(ctx, opts) {
    const o = opts || {};
    const t = tools(ctx), px = t.px, rect = t.rect;
    const rnd = makeRnd(o.compound ? 77 : 31);
    stoneWall(px, rect, 176);
    const win = function (x, y) {
      rect(x - 3, y - 3, 30, 44, '#8b8177'); rect(x - 3, y - 3, 30, 1, '#a49a8e'); rect(x, y + 4, 24, 36, '#5fa4de'); rect(x + 4, y, 16, 4, '#5fa4de'); rect(x + 2, y + 2, 20, 2, '#5fa4de'); rect(x, y + 22, 24, 18, '#7bb8e8');
      rect(x + 2, y + 10, 8, 2, '#fbf9f2'); if (!o.compound) rect(x + 4, y + 9, 5, 1, '#fbf9f2'); rect(x + 12, y + 16, 9, 2, '#fbf9f2'); rect(x + 11, y + 4, 2, 36, '#3f3934'); rect(x, y + 20, 24, 2, '#3f3934');
    };
    win(48, 56); win(312, 56);
    torchSmall(ctx, px, rect, 130, 72, 7); torchSmall(ctx, px, rect, 254, 72, 7);
    stoneFloor(px, rect, rnd, 176, 400);
    for (let r = 0; r < 4; r++) for (let c = 0; c < 9 - r * 2; c++) { const x = 8 + c * 14, y = 236 + r * 12; rect(x, y, 13, 11, (r + c) & 1 ? '#5d8fd8' : '#f6efd9'); rect(x, y, 13, 1, (r + c) & 1 ? '#8fb3ea' : '#fbf9f2'); rect(x, y + 10, 13, 1, '#2f3f75'); rect(x + 12, y, 1, 11, '#2f3f75'); }
    if (!o.compound) for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) { const x = 300 + i * 12, y = 262 + (i & 1) * 6; rect(x, y, 13, 11, i & 1 ? '#5d8fd8' : '#f6efd9'); rect(x, y + 10, 13, 1, '#2f3f75'); }
  }

  // ---- 解説（黒板のある部屋）----
  function explain(ctx) {
    const t = tools(ctx), px = t.px, rect = t.rect;
    const rnd = makeRnd(143);
    stoneWall(px, rect, 176);
    rect(120, 40, 144, 92, '#2b3a32'); rect(117, 37, 150, 3, '#8a6440'); rect(117, 132, 150, 4, '#8a6440');
    for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) rect(132 + i * 40, 60 + i * 18, 24 + i * 6, 2, '#7d8f86');
    torchSmall(ctx, px, rect, 96, 76, 8); torchSmall(ctx, px, rect, 288, 76, 8);
    stoneFloor(px, rect, rnd, 176, 400);
  }

  // ---- やり直し（踊り場の扉）----
  function redo(ctx) {
    const t = tools(ctx), px = t.px, rect = t.rect;
    const rnd = makeRnd(91);
    stoneWall(px, rect, 176);
    const door = function (x, y) {
      rect(x - 3, y - 3, 42, 79, '#a49a8e'); rect(x - 3, y - 3, 42, 1, '#b8ae9f');
      rect(x + 12, y, 12, 1, '#0f0f1a'); rect(x + 8, y + 1, 20, 1, '#0f0f1a'); rect(x + 5, y + 2, 26, 2, '#0f0f1a'); rect(x + 3, y + 4, 30, 72, '#2a2a3c');
      for (let i = 0; i < 30; i += 6) rect(x + 3, y + 8 + i, 30, 1, '#1c1c30');
      rect(x + 16, y + 40, 4, 5, '#ffd37c');
    };
    door(172, 96);
    torchSmall(ctx, px, rect, 112, 84, 8); torchSmall(ctx, px, rect, 272, 84, 8);
    stoneFloor(px, rect, rnd, 176, 400);
  }

  // ---- 結果（上の階への階段）----
  function result(ctx) {
    const t = tools(ctx), px = t.px, rect = t.rect;
    const rnd = makeRnd(205);
    stoneWall(px, rect, 168);
    for (let i = 0; i < 7; i++) { const w = 150 - i * 6, x = 192 - w / 2, y = 168 - i * 10; rect(x, y, w, 10, i & 1 ? '#8b8177' : '#7b7168'); rect(x, y, w, 2, '#a49a8e'); rect(x, y + 9, w, 1, '#4f4941'); }
    torchSmall(ctx, px, rect, 84, 74, 8); torchSmall(ctx, px, rect, 300, 74, 8);
    stoneFloor(px, rect, rnd, 168, 380);
  }

  // ---- ホーム（塔の外観）----
  const HOME = {
    G: 252, U: 6, FH: 24,
    span: { 1: [1, 25], 2: [1, 25], 3: [3, 23], 4: [3, 23], 5: [5, 21], 6: [5, 21], 7: [5, 21], 8: [3, 23], 9: [3, 23], 10: [3, 23], 11: [3, 23], 12: [7, 19], 13: [7, 19], 14: [8, 18] }
  };
  function hcx(c) { return 192 - 12 * HOME.U + (c - 1) * HOME.U; }
  function hyTop(n) { return HOME.G - n * HOME.FH; }
  function homePlaques() { return [[hcx(3) + 5, hyTop(4) + 16, 36, 16], [hcx(21) - 40, hyTop(7) + 14, 36, 16], [hcx(3) + 5, hyTop(9) + 4, 36, 16]]; }
  function homeSign() { return [170, 206, 44, 8]; }

  function home(ctx, opts) {
    const o = opts || {};
    const t = tools(ctx), px = t.px, rect = t.rect;
    const G = HOME.G, FH = HOME.FH, span = HOME.span, cx = hcx, yTop = hyTop;
    const cur = o.cur || 1, cleared = o.cleared || 0;
    const rnd = makeRnd(20260913);
    // 空（バンド＋ディザ）
    const bands = [[0, '#4a92d4'], [46, '#5fa4de'], [96, '#7bb8e8'], [140, '#9ccdef'], [176, '#bde0f5']];
    rect(0, 0, W, H, bands[0][1]);
    bands.forEach(function (b, i) { const y = b[0], c = b[1]; if (!i) return; rect(0, y, W, H - y, c); for (let yy = y - 5; yy < y; yy++) for (let x = (yy & 1); x < W; x += 2) if (rnd() < 0.6) px(x, yy, c); });
    // 雲
    const cloud = function (x, y, w) { const oc = '#8fb9dc'; rect(x - 1, y + 2, w + 2, 5, oc); rect(x + 2, y, w - 4, 2, oc); rect(x + (w * 0.3 | 0) - 1, y - 2, (w * 0.36 | 0) + 2, 2, oc); rect(x + (w * 0.58 | 0) - 1, y - 1, (w * 0.24 | 0) + 2, 1, oc); rect(x, y + 7, w, 2, oc); rect(x, y + 3, w, 3, '#fbf9f2'); rect(x + 3, y + 1, w - 6, 2, '#fbf9f2'); rect(x + (w * 0.3 | 0), y - 1, w * 0.36 | 0, 2, '#fbf9f2'); rect(x + (w * 0.58 | 0), y, w * 0.24 | 0, 1, '#fbf9f2'); rect(x + 1, y + 6, w - 2, 1, '#d5e6f0'); rect(x + 3, y + 7, w - 6, 1, '#c3dbea'); };
    cloud(28, 40, 40); cloud(96, 22, 26); cloud(262, 30, 48); cloud(330, 62, 30); cloud(150, 70, 22); cloud(300, 96, 20);
    // 山（遠・近）
    const ridge = function (pts, c, hl, snow, base) {
      for (let x = 0; x < W; x++) {
        let i = 0; while (i < pts.length - 2 && pts[i + 1][0] < x) i++;
        const x0 = pts[i][0], y0 = pts[i][1], x1 = pts[i + 1][0], y1 = pts[i + 1][1], tt = (x - x0) / (x1 - x0), y = Math.round(y0 + (y1 - y0) * tt);
        rect(x, y, 1, base - y, c);
        if (y1 < y0) for (let yy = y; yy < Math.min(base, y + 14); yy++) if (((x + yy) & 1) === 0 && rnd() < 0.7) px(x, yy, hl);
        if (snow) for (let yy = y; yy < Math.min(snow, base); yy++) if (yy < snow - 3 || ((x + yy) & 1) === 0) px(x, yy, '#e6ecf4');
        px(x, y, hl);
      }
    };
    ridge([[0, 152], [28, 118], [56, 142], [92, 102], [124, 136], [154, 122], [192, 150], [232, 114], [262, 140], [304, 98], [334, 130], [362, 112], [384, 140]], '#6f7ea8', '#8c98bc', 118, 198);
    ridge([[0, 176], [40, 150], [80, 172], [112, 144], [150, 174], [192, 182], [236, 166], [278, 146], [320, 170], [350, 152], [384, 174]], '#556085', '#6b7699', 0, 198);
    // 湖・対岸
    rect(0, 196, W, 2, '#4f8a5e'); rect(0, 198, W, 18, '#3b7fc8');
    for (let i = 0; i < 70; i++) { const x = rnd() * W | 0, y = 199 + (rnd() * 16 | 0); rect(x, y, 2 + (rnd() * 3 | 0), 1, y % 2 ? '#6ea8e0' : '#2f6fb5'); }
    // 草地
    rect(0, 214, W, H - 214, '#5aa15a'); rect(0, 214, W, 2, '#7cc06b');
    for (let i = 0; i < 900; i++) { const x = rnd() * W | 0, y = 216 + (rnd() * 72 | 0); px(x, y, rnd() < 0.5 ? '#4f9a4e' : '#69b262'); }
    // 木
    const tree = function (tcx, tcy, r, palT) {
      const mid = palT[0], light = palT[1], dark = palT[2];
      for (let dy = -r - 1; dy <= r + 1; dy++) { const hw = Math.round(Math.sqrt(Math.max(0, (r + 1) * (r + 1) - dy * dy))); rect(tcx - hw, tcy + dy, hw * 2 + 1, 1, '#1f3a2a'); }
      rect(tcx - 2, tcy + r, 4, 4, '#1f3a2a');
      for (let dy = -r; dy <= r; dy++) { const hw = Math.round(Math.sqrt(r * r - dy * dy)); for (let dx = -hw; dx <= hw; dx++) { let c = mid; if (dy > r * 0.45 || (dx >= hw - 1 && dy > -r * 0.3)) c = dark; else if (dx < -r * 0.15 && dy < -r * 0.1 && ((dx + dy) & 1) === 0) c = light; if (Math.abs(dx) === hw && Math.abs(dy) > r * 0.6) c = dark; px(tcx + dx, tcy + dy, c); } }
      rect(tcx - 1, tcy + r, 2, 3, '#5a3a22'); px(tcx, tcy + r + 1, '#3b2616');
    };
    const pathHalf = function (y) { return y < 252 ? 14 : 14 + (y - 252) * 0.7; };
    const backPal = ['#2f6a4a', '#3f8558', '#254f38'], frontPal = ['#4f9a4e', '#7cc06b', '#2f6a4a'], midPal = ['#3d8a55', '#5fb06a', '#2a5a3f'];
    for (let i = 0; i < 46; i++) { const x = rnd() * W | 0, y = 206 + (rnd() * 14 | 0), r = 5 + (rnd() * 3 | 0); if (Math.abs(x - 192) < 70) continue; tree(x, y, r, backPal); }
    for (let i = 0; i < 30; i++) { const x = rnd() * W | 0, y = 224 + (rnd() * 14 | 0), r = 6 + (rnd() * 3 | 0); if (Math.abs(x - 192) < 62 + r) continue; tree(x, y, r, midPal); }
    // 塔の影
    rect(cx(1) - 6, G, cx(25) - cx(1) + 12, 3, '#3d7a45');
    // 石壁
    const wall = function (x, y, w, h) {
      rect(x, y, w, h, '#8b8177');
      for (let yy = y; yy < y + h; yy++) {
        const row = Math.floor(yy / 4); if (yy % 4 === 3) { rect(x, yy, w, 1, '#4a433c'); continue; }
        const off = (row & 1) * 4;
        for (let xx = x; xx < x + w; xx++) {
          if (((xx + off) % 8) === 0) { px(xx, yy, '#4a433c'); continue; }
          const bi = ((xx + off) >> 3) * 131 + row * 17, v = (Math.imul(bi, 2654435761) >>> 0) % 9;
          if (v === 0) px(xx, yy, '#9c928a'); else if (v === 1) px(xx, yy, '#7b7168'); else if (v === 2 && yy % 4 === 1) px(xx, yy, '#a49a8e');
          if (yy % 4 === 0) px(xx, yy, v < 4 ? '#a49a8e' : '#988e84');
        }
      }
    };
    for (let n = 1; n <= 14; n++) { const a = span[n][0], b = span[n][1]; rect(cx(a) - 1, yTop(n) - 1, cx(b) - cx(a) + 2, FH + 2, '#1e1d2a'); }
    for (let n = 1; n <= 14; n++) {
      const a = span[n][0], b = span[n][1], xa = cx(a), xb = cx(b), yt = yTop(n);
      wall(xa, yt, xb - xa, FH);
      rect(xa, yt, 1, FH, '#a49a8e'); rect(xb - 2, yt, 2, FH, '#6d655c'); rect(xb - 1, yt, 1, FH, '#5c554d');
    }
    // 段差のひさし・持ち送り
    for (let n = 2; n <= 14; n++) {
      const a = span[n][0], b = span[n][1], pa = span[n - 1][0], pb = span[n - 1][1], y = yTop(n - 1);
      if (a > pa) { rect(cx(pa) - 1, y - 1, cx(pb) - cx(pa) + 2, 2, '#b8ae9f'); rect(cx(pa) - 1, y + 1, cx(pb) - cx(pa) + 2, 1, '#4f4941'); }
      if (a < pa) { const y2 = yTop(n) + FH; rect(cx(a), y2 - 1, cx(b) - cx(a), 1, '#4f4941'); for (let x = cx(a) + 2; x < cx(pa); x += 5) rect(x, y2, 2, 3, '#6d655c'); for (let x = cx(pb) + 1; x < cx(b) - 2; x += 5) rect(x, y2, 2, 3, '#6d655c'); }
    }
    rect(cx(1) - 3, G - 6, cx(25) - cx(1) + 6, 6, '#7b7168'); rect(cx(1) - 3, G - 6, cx(25) - cx(1) + 6, 1, '#a49a8e'); rect(cx(1) - 3, G - 3, cx(25) - cx(1) + 6, 1, '#5c554d');
    for (let x = cx(1) - 3; x < cx(25) + 3; x += 9) rect(x, G - 2, 1, 2, '#5c554d');
    // 窓・たいまつ（クリアした階と いまの階の まどが 光る）
    const glows = [];
    const win = function (x, y, lit) { rect(x - 1, y - 1, 5, 11, '#a49a8e'); px(x - 1, y - 1, '#8b8177'); px(x + 3, y - 1, '#8b8177'); rect(x, y + 1, 3, 9, lit ? '#e8842a' : '#151526'); px(x + 1, y, lit ? '#e8842a' : '#151526'); rect(x - 1, y + 10, 5, 1, '#6d655c'); if (lit) { rect(x + 1, y + 2, 1, 7, '#ffd37c'); px(x + 1, y + 4, '#fff3c4'); glows.push([x + 1, y + 5, 5, 0.18]); } else { px(x + 2, y + 2, '#2c2c48'); px(x + 1, y + 5, '#2c2c48'); } };
    const torch = function (x, y, big) { rect(x, y + 2, 1, big ? 5 : 3, '#5a3a22'); rect(x - 1, y, 3, 2, '#f4a23c'); px(x, y - 1, '#ffd37c'); px(x, y, '#ffe7a3'); if (big) { px(x - 1, y - 1, '#e8842a'); px(x + 1, y - 1, '#e8842a'); } glows.push([x, y, big ? 7 : 5, 0.28]); };
    for (let n = 2; n <= 14; n++) {
      const a = span[n][0], b = span[n][1], xa = cx(a) + 2, xb = cx(b) - 3, yt = yTop(n), lit = n <= cleared || n === cur;
      const cnt = Math.max(1, Math.round((xb - xa) / 22));
      for (let i = 0; i < cnt; i++) { const c = Math.round(xa + (xb - xa) * (i + 1) / (cnt + 1)); if (Math.abs(c - 192) < 22 && n <= 2) continue; win(c - 1, yt + 7, lit); }
      if ([3, 6, 9, 13].indexOf(n) >= 0) { torch(cx(a) + 4, yt + 10, false); torch(cx(b) - 5, yt + 10, false); }
    }
    // 十字の張り出しの小屋根
    const roofF = function (x, y, w) { for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) rect(x + i, y - i - 1, w - i * 2, 1, i ? '#3a4c8a' : '#2f3f75'); px(x + (w >> 1), y - 5, '#2f3f75'); };
    roofF(cx(3) - 2, yTop(4), 12); roofF(cx(23) - 10, yTop(4), 12);
    roofF(cx(1) - 2, yTop(2), 12); roofF(cx(25) - 10, yTop(2), 12);
    // 上のほうは空気遠近でかすませる（てっぺんは画面の外）
    for (let y = 0; y < 60; y++) { const pp = (60 - y) / 60 * 0.4; for (let x = cx(3) - 3; x < cx(23) + 3; x++) if (rnd() < pp * pp) px(x, y, '#4a92d4'); }
    ctx.globalAlpha = 0.96; cloud(214, 36, 40); ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
    // 入口
    const dx = 180, dy = 218, dh = G - dy;
    rect(dx - 3, dy - 3, 30, dh + 3, '#a49a8e'); rect(dx - 3, dy - 3, 30, 1, '#b8ae9f'); rect(dx - 3, dy - 3, 1, dh + 3, '#b8ae9f'); rect(dx + 26, dy - 3, 1, dh + 3, '#8b8177');
    for (let x = dx - 3; x < dx + 27; x += 6) px(x, dy - 1, '#8b8177');
    rect(dx + 8, dy, 8, 1, '#0f0f1a'); rect(dx + 5, dy + 1, 14, 1, '#0f0f1a'); rect(dx + 3, dy + 2, 18, 1, '#0f0f1a'); rect(dx + 2, dy + 3, 20, 2, '#0f0f1a'); rect(dx + 1, dy + 5, 22, dh - 5, '#0f0f1a');
    for (let i = 0; i < 60; i++) px(dx + 2 + (rnd() * 20 | 0), dy + 7 + (rnd() * (dh - 9) | 0), '#1c1c30');
    rect(dx + 1, G - 2, 22, 2, '#2a2a3c');
    const door = function (x, flip) { const h = dh - 6; rect(x, dy + 6, 7, h, '#7a4a2a'); for (let i = 0; i < 7; i += 2) rect(x + i, dy + 6, 1, h, '#5a3a22'); rect(x, dy + 10, 7, 1, '#3b2616'); rect(x, dy + h - 1, 7, 1, '#3b2616'); px(flip ? x + 1 : x + 5, dy + 18, '#ffd37c'); };
    door(dx - 9, false); door(dx + 25, true);
    torch(dx - 15, dy + 13, true); torch(dx + 38, dy + 13, true);
    // 看板（木）
    homePlaques().forEach(function (bx) { const x = bx[0], y = bx[1], w = bx[2], h = bx[3]; rect(x, y, w, h, '#6b4a2e'); rect(x, y, w, 1, '#8a6440'); rect(x, y, 1, h, '#8a6440'); rect(x, y + h - 1, w, 1, '#3a2616'); rect(x + w - 1, y, 1, h, '#3a2616'); px(x + 1, y + 1, '#3a2616'); px(x + w - 2, y + 1, '#3a2616'); px(x + 1, y + h - 2, '#3a2616'); px(x + w - 2, y + h - 2, '#3a2616'); rect(x + 3, y - 3, 1, 3, '#3a2616'); rect(x + w - 4, y - 3, 1, 3, '#3a2616'); });
    const sb = homeSign(); rect(sb[0], sb[1], sb[2], sb[3], '#e8dcb5'); rect(sb[0], sb[1], sb[2], 1, '#fbf9f2'); rect(sb[0], sb[1] + sb[3] - 1, sb[2], 1, '#7a5c3a'); rect(sb[0], sb[1], 1, sb[3], '#7a5c3a'); rect(sb[0] + sb[2] - 1, sb[1], 1, sb[3], '#7a5c3a');
    // 階段・石畳
    for (let y = G; y < G + 10; y++) { const hw = 15 + (y - G); rect(192 - hw, y, hw * 2, 1, (y - G) % 3 === 2 ? '#8c877a' : '#c9c3b3'); px(192 - hw, y, '#6e6b85'); px(192 + hw - 1, y, '#6e6b85'); }
    for (let y = G + 10; y < H; y++) { const hw = Math.round(pathHalf(y)) + 10; rect(192 - hw, y, hw * 2, 1, y % 6 === 0 ? '#9a8f7c' : '#b8ad96'); if (y % 6 !== 0) for (let x = 192 - hw; x < 192 + hw; x++) if (((x + ((y / 6 | 0) & 1) * 4) % 8) === 0) px(x, y, '#9a8f7c'); rect(192 - hw, y, 2, 1, '#6e6b85'); rect(192 + hw - 2, y, 2, 1, '#6e6b85'); }
    // 手前の木・しげみ
    for (let i = 0; i < 22; i++) { const x = rnd() * W | 0, y = 250 + (rnd() * 26 | 0), r = 6 + (rnd() * 4 | 0); if (Math.abs(x - 192) < pathHalf(y) + 14 + r) continue; tree(x, y, r, frontPal); }
    for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) { const x = rnd() * W | 0, y = 246 + (rnd() * 8 | 0), r = 3 + (rnd() * 2 | 0); if (Math.abs(x - 192) < 40) continue; tree(x, y, r, midPal); }
    // 主人公（後ろ姿）
    rect(183, 267, 18, 1, 'rgba(0,0,0,0.3)');
    const S = root.MT.Sprites;
    pixelsOf(S.HERO_BACK, S.HERO_PAL).out.forEach(function (p) { px(182 + p[0], 241 + p[1], p[2]); });
    // 明かりのにじみ
    glows.forEach(function (gl) { const x = gl[0], y = gl[1], r = gl[2], a = gl[3]; ctx.globalAlpha = a; for (let ddy = -r; ddy <= r; ddy++) for (let ddx = -r; ddx <= r; ddx++) if (Math.abs(ddx) + Math.abs(ddy) <= r && ((ddx + ddy) & 1) === 0) px(x + ddx, y + ddy, '#ffb347'); ctx.globalAlpha = 1; });
  }

  // ---- 屋上 ----
  function sky(rect, px, rnd) {
    const bands = [[0, '#2f3f75'], [26, '#4a4a8f'], [52, '#7b5f9e'], [78, '#c2669a'], [100, '#ef8a5c'], [118, '#ffb347'], [132, '#ffd37c']];
    rect(0, 0, 384, 150, bands[0][1]);
    bands.forEach(function (b, i) { const y = b[0], c = b[1]; if (!i) return; rect(0, y, 384, 150 - y, c); for (let yy = y - 5; yy < y; yy++) for (let x = (yy & 1); x < 384; x += 2) if (rnd() < 0.6) px(x, yy, c); });
    for (let i = 0; i < 70; i++) { const x = rnd() * 384 | 0, y = rnd() * 60 | 0; if (rnd() < 0.5) px(x, y, '#fbf9f2'); }
  }

  function chestSprite(ctx, px, rect, rnd, open) {
    const Wc = 96, Hc = 72, ox = 144, oy = 178;
    const m = {}, set = function (x, y, c) { if (x >= 0 && x < Wc && y >= 0 && y < Hc) m[x + ',' + y] = c; };
    const bodyTop = 30, bodyH = 34;
    for (let y = 0; y < bodyH; y++) {
      const inset = Math.round((1 - y / bodyH) * 2), x0 = 4 + inset, x1 = Wc - 4 - inset, yy = bodyTop + y;
      for (let x = x0; x < x1; x++) {
        let c = '#7a4a2a';
        if ((x - x0) % 10 < 1) c = '#5a3a22'; else if ((x - x0) % 10 < 3) c = '#8f5a32';
        if (y > bodyH - 5) c = '#4a2c18';
        set(x, yy, c);
      }
      for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) { set(x0 + i, yy, i < 3 ? '#f4a23c' : '#c2661f'); set(x1 - 1 - i, yy, i < 2 ? '#c2661f' : '#f4a23c'); }
    }
    [bodyTop + 4, bodyTop + 22].forEach(function (y) { for (let x = 2; x < Wc - 2; x++) { set(x, y, '#ffd37c'); set(x, y + 1, '#f4a23c'); set(x, y + 2, '#c2661f'); } for (let x = 6; x < Wc - 6; x += 12) { set(x, y + 1, '#ffe7a3'); set(x + 1, y + 1, '#8a4a10'); } });
    // ふた：閉＝手前にドーム／開＝うしろへ倒れて浅く見える
    const lidH = open ? 14 : 26, lidY = open ? bodyTop - 17 : bodyTop - lidH + 2;
    for (let y = 0; y < lidH; y++) {
      const tt = y / lidH;
      const inset = Math.round(Math.pow(open ? tt : 1 - tt, 1.6) * (open ? 8 : 12)), yy = lidY + y;
      for (let x = 4 + inset; x < Wc - 4 - inset; x++) {
        let c = '#8f5a32';
        if (open) c = y < 3 ? '#5a3a22' : y > lidH - 4 ? '#a06a3a' : '#7a4a2a';
        else if (y < 3) c = '#a06a3a'; else if (y > lidH - 4) c = '#5a3a22';
        set(x, yy, c);
      }
      if (y > 1 && y < lidH - 1) { for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) { set(4 + inset + i, yy, '#f4a23c'); set(Wc - 5 - inset - i, yy, '#c2661f'); } }
    }
    (open ? [0.3, 0.62] : [0.32, 0.56, 0.8]).forEach(function (f) {
      const y = lidY + Math.round(lidH * f), inset = Math.round(Math.pow(open ? f : 1 - f, 1.6) * (open ? 8 : 12));
      for (let x = 4 + inset; x < Wc - 4 - inset; x++) { set(x, y, '#ffd37c'); set(x, y + 1, '#c2661f'); }
    });
    if (!open) [[-26, '#e2584c'], [0, '#5fa4de'], [26, '#6cbd6f']].forEach(function (g) {
      const gcx = Wc / 2 + g[0], gcy = lidY + Math.round(lidH * 0.62), c = g[1];
      for (let ddy = -3; ddy <= 3; ddy++) { const hw = 3 - Math.abs(ddy); for (let x = -hw; x <= hw; x++) set(gcx + x, gcy + ddy, ddy < 0 ? '#fbf9f2' : c); }
      set(gcx, gcy - 1, '#fbf9f2');
    });
    if (open) {
      for (let y = 0; y < 10; y++) for (let x = 8; x < Wc - 8; x++) set(x, bodyTop - 2 + y, y < 2 ? '#2a1c10' : '#3b2616');
      [12, Wc - 14].forEach(function (x) { for (let y = 0; y < 5; y++) { set(x, bodyTop - 4 + y, '#ffd37c'); set(x + 1, bodyTop - 4 + y, '#c2661f'); } });
    }
    for (let y = 0; y < 16; y++) for (let x = -8; x < 8; x++) set(Wc / 2 + x, bodyTop + 10 + y, Math.abs(x) > 6 || y > 13 ? '#c2661f' : '#ffd37c');
    for (let y = 4; y < 11; y++) for (let x = -3; x < 3; x++) set(Wc / 2 + x, bodyTop + 10 + y, '#3b2616');
    set(Wc / 2 - 1, bodyTop + 16, '#5fa4de'); set(Wc / 2, bodyTop + 16, '#8fb3ea');
    const out = [];
    Object.keys(m).forEach(function (k) { const xy = k.split(',').map(Number); out.push([xy[0], xy[1], m[k]]); });
    const has = function (x, y) { return !!m[x + ',' + y]; };
    for (let y = -1; y <= Hc; y++) for (let x = -1; x <= Wc; x++) if (!has(x, y) && (has(x - 1, y) || has(x + 1, y) || has(x, y - 1) || has(x, y + 1))) out.push([x, y, '#1e1d2a']);
    if (open) { ctx.globalAlpha = 0.18; for (let a = 0; a <= 16; a++) { const th = Math.PI + a / 16 * Math.PI; for (let r = 10; r < 150; r += 2) px((ox + Wc / 2 + Math.cos(th) * r) | 0, (oy + 26 + Math.sin(th) * r * 0.85) | 0, a & 1 ? '#ffe7a3' : '#ffd37c'); } ctx.globalAlpha = 1; }
    rect(ox + 2, oy + 64, Wc - 4, 4, '#5c554d');
    out.forEach(function (p) { px(ox + p[0], oy + p[1], p[2]); });
    if (open) {
      for (let i = 0; i < 30; i++) { const x = ox + 12 + (rnd() * (Wc - 24) | 0), y = oy + 26 + (rnd() * 8 | 0); rect(x, y, 4, 3, '#ffd37c'); px(x + 1, y, '#ffe7a3'); px(x, y + 2, '#c2661f'); }
      for (let i = 0; i < 20; i++) { const x = ox - 20 + (rnd() * (Wc + 40) | 0), y = oy + 60 + (rnd() * 12 | 0); rect(x, y, 5, 3, '#ffd37c'); px(x + 1, y, '#ffe7a3'); px(x + 4, y + 2, '#c2661f'); }
    }
  }

  function roofChest(ctx, opts) {
    const o = opts || {};
    const t = tools(ctx), px = t.px, rect = t.rect;
    const rnd = makeRnd(7);
    sky(rect, px, rnd);
    for (let y = 148; y < 162; y++) {
      const tt = (y - 148) / 14;
      for (let x = 0; x < 384; x++) {
        const warm = Math.sin(x / 47) * 0.5 + 0.5;
        px(x, y, tt < 0.35 ? (warm > 0.5 ? '#f6d0b0' : '#efc0a4') : tt < 0.7 ? (warm > 0.45 ? '#e6d2e4' : '#dfc3d6') : (warm > 0.5 ? '#d99a86' : '#cf8f84'));
      }
    }
    for (let x = 0; x < 384; x++) {
      const top = 148 - 4 - Math.round(Math.sin(x / 23) * 3);
      for (let y = top; y < 148; y++) if (((x + y) & 1) === 0 && rnd() < (y - top + 1) / 6) px(x, y, y < 145 ? '#f6d0b0' : '#efc0a4');
      if (rnd() < 0.4) px(x, 148 - Math.round(Math.sin(x / 17) * 2), '#f6d0b0');
    }
    const tones = ['#fbf1e2', '#f6d0b0', '#e6d2e4', '#d99a86'];
    for (let i = 0; i < 120; i++) {
      const y = 146 + (rnd() * 15 | 0), len = 8 + (rnd() * 32 | 0), x0 = rnd() * 384 | 0, c = tones[rnd() * 4 | 0];
      for (let x = x0; x < x0 + len; x++) { px(x, y, c); if (rnd() < 0.35) px(x, y + 1, c); }
    }
    rect(0, 160, 384, 128, '#7b7168'); rect(0, 160, 384, 3, '#a49a8e');
    let fy = 162, gap = 5;
    while (fy < 288) {
      rect(0, fy, 384, 1, '#5c554d'); rect(0, fy + 1, 384, 1, '#8b8177');
      const step = Math.round(48 + gap * 2);
      for (let x = ((gap & 1) ? 0 : step / 2); x < 384; x += step) rect(x, fy, 2, gap, '#5c554d');
      fy += gap; gap += 3;
    }
    for (let x = 0; x < 384; x += 28) { rect(x, 140, 18, 22, '#8b8177'); rect(x, 140, 18, 2, '#a49a8e'); rect(x + 16, 140, 2, 22, '#6d655c'); }
    chestSprite(ctx, px, rect, rnd, !!o.opened);
    // 地下への扉（床のハッチ）
    rect(40, 240, 56, 30, '#5c554d'); rect(40, 240, 56, 3, '#8b8177'); rect(44, 246, 48, 22, '#3b2616');
    for (let x = 48; x < 92; x += 8) rect(x, 246, 3, 22, '#2a1c10');
    rect(64, 254, 8, 6, '#ffd37c');
  }

  function roofView(ctx) {
    const t = tools(ctx), px = t.px, rect = t.rect;
    const rnd = makeRnd(21);
    sky(rect, px, rnd);
    const sx = 192, sy = 100, sr = 30;
    ctx.globalAlpha = 0.22; for (let dy = -sr * 2; dy <= sr * 2; dy++) { const hw = Math.round(Math.sqrt(Math.max(0, sr * sr * 4 - dy * dy))); for (let x = -hw; x <= hw; x += 2) if (((x + dy) & 1) === 0) px(sx + x, sy + dy, '#ffd37c'); } ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
    for (let dy = -sr; dy <= sr; dy++) { const hw = Math.round(Math.sqrt(Math.max(0, sr * sr - dy * dy))); rect(sx - hw, sy + dy, hw * 2, 1, dy < -sr * 0.3 ? '#fff3c4' : '#ffe7a3'); }
    [[30, 92, 90], [210, 84, 120], [120, 106, 70], [300, 112, 60]].forEach(function (c) {
      const x = c[0], y = c[1], w = c[2];
      rect(x, y, w, 3, '#f6d0b0'); rect(x + 6, y - 2, w - 14, 2, '#f6d0b0'); rect(x + 2, y + 3, w - 6, 2, '#d99a86');
    });
    const ridge = function (pts, c, hl, base) {
      for (let x = 0; x < 384; x++) {
        let i = 0; while (i < pts.length - 2 && pts[i + 1][0] < x) i++;
        const x0 = pts[i][0], y0 = pts[i][1], x1 = pts[i + 1][0], y1 = pts[i + 1][1], tt = (x - x0) / (x1 - x0), y = Math.round(y0 + (y1 - y0) * tt);
        rect(x, y, 1, base - y, c); px(x, y, hl);
      }
    };
    ridge([[0, 130], [46, 112], [96, 130], [150, 106], [212, 128], [268, 110], [322, 130], [384, 118]], '#7a6b9c', '#9a8ab4', 158);
    ridge([[0, 140], [60, 128], [130, 140], [200, 126], [280, 141], [340, 132], [384, 140]], '#5f5580', '#7a6d96', 158);
    rect(0, 156, 384, 18, '#4a6fa8'); rect(0, 156, 384, 1, '#8fb3ea');
    for (let y = 157; y < 174; y++) {
      const spread = 14 + (y - 157) * 2.6;
      for (let k = 0; k < 5; k++) {
        const off = (rnd() * 2 - 1) * spread, len = 2 + (rnd() * 7 | 0);
        const x0 = Math.round(sx + off - len / 2);
        if (rnd() > 1 - Math.abs(off) / (spread + 8)) continue;
        for (let x = x0; x < x0 + len; x++) px(x, y, rnd() < 0.5 ? '#ffd37c' : '#ffe7a3');
      }
    }
    for (let y = 158; y < 174; y += 3) for (let x = 0; x < 384; x += 2) px(x + (y & 1), y, '#41649a');
    rect(0, 174, 384, 114, '#4f9a4e');
    for (let y = 174; y < 240; y += 6) rect(0, y, 384, 3, '#458a46');
    rect(0, 174, 384, 2, '#7cc06b');
    for (let y = 174; y < 230; y++) {
      const tt = y - 174, w = Math.round(4 + tt * 0.6), x = Math.round(sx + 30 - tt * 0.7 + Math.sin(tt / 9) * 6);
      rect(x, y, w, 1, '#4a86c8'); px(x, y, '#2f6aa8'); px(x + w - 1, y, '#2f6aa8');
      if (((y + (x % 5)) % 6) === 0) rect(x + 2, y, Math.max(1, w - 4), 1, '#8fb3ea');
    }
    const castle = function (ccx, ccy) {
      rect(ccx - 34, ccy, 68, 22, '#e8dcb5'); rect(ccx - 34, ccy, 68, 2, '#fbf9f2'); rect(ccx - 34, ccy + 20, 68, 2, '#a8a08c');
      for (let x = ccx - 34; x < ccx + 34; x += 6) rect(x, ccy - 3, 4, 3, '#e8dcb5');
      [[-30, 16], [-10, 24], [12, 20], [28, 14]].forEach(function (tw) {
        const x = ccx + tw[0], h = tw[1], y = ccy - h;
        rect(x - 5, y, 11, h + 22, '#f1e4c3'); rect(x + 4, y, 2, h + 22, '#c9c3b3');
        for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) rect(x - 5 - i, y - 5 + i, 11 + i * 2, 1, '#a8443a');
        px(x, y - 8, '#ffd37c'); rect(x, y - 8, 5, 3, '#e2584c');
        rect(x - 2, y + 8, 3, 4, '#5a6f9a');
      });
      rect(ccx - 6, ccy + 10, 8, 12, '#7a4a2a');
    };
    for (let x = 264; x < 384; x++) { const y = 176 - Math.round(Math.sin((x - 264) / 120 * Math.PI) * 18); rect(x, y, 1, 240 - y, '#458a46'); px(x, y, '#7cc06b'); }
    castle(320, 148);
    const roofs = ['#a8443a', '#8c3a32', '#b8583a', '#7a4a2a'], walls = ['#e8dcb5', '#f1e4c3', '#d9cCae', '#e0d2a8'];
    for (let i = 0; i < 24; i++) {
      const w = 8 + (rnd() * 5 | 0), h = 6 + (rnd() * 5 | 0);
      const x = 248 + (rnd() * 118 | 0), y = 182 + (rnd() * 26 | 0);
      rect(x, y, w, h, walls[i % 4]); rect(x, y - 3, w, 3, roofs[i % 4]); px(x, y - 3, '#6b2a24');
      rect(x + w - 3, y + h - 4, 2, 4, '#8a6440');
      if (rnd() < 0.3) rect(x + 2, y + 2, 2, 2, '#5a6f9a');
    }
    for (let i = 0; i < 8; i++) { const x = 246 + (rnd() * 120 | 0), y = 186 + (rnd() * 24 | 0); for (let dy = -3; dy <= 3; dy++) { const hw = Math.round(Math.sqrt(9 - dy * dy)); for (let dx = -hw; dx <= hw; dx++) px(x + dx, y + dy, dy > 1 ? '#27573d' : '#3f8558'); } }
    const tree = function (tcx, tcy, r, dark) { for (let dy = -r; dy <= r; dy++) { const hw = Math.round(Math.sqrt(r * r - dy * dy)); for (let dx = -hw; dx <= hw; dx++) px(tcx + dx, tcy + dy, dy > r * 0.35 ? (dark ? '#1b4030' : '#2f6a4a') : (dark ? '#27573d' : '#3f8558')); } rect(tcx - 1, tcy + r, 2, 4, '#4a2f18'); };
    for (let i = 0; i < 30; i++) tree(6 + (rnd() * 240 | 0), 192 + (rnd() * 12 | 0), 4 + (rnd() * 2 | 0), false);
    rect(0, 224, 384, 20, '#27573d');
    for (let i = 0; i < 30; i++) tree(rnd() * 384 | 0, 224 + (rnd() * 10 | 0), 7 + (rnd() * 3 | 0), true);
    [[96, 162], [140, 168], [286, 165]].forEach(function (b) { const x = b[0], y = b[1]; rect(x, y, 9, 2, '#5a3a22'); rect(x + 4, y - 8, 1, 8, '#3b2616'); for (let i = 0; i < 7; i++) rect(x + 5, y - 8 + i, 1 + i, 1, i < 4 ? '#fbf9f2' : '#e8dcb5'); });
    for (let y = 178; y < 238; y++) { const w = 2 + (y - 178) * 0.22, x = 96 + (y - 178) * 1.1; rect(x, y, w, 1, '#c9b98c'); }
    [[108, 196], [128, 214], [150, 232]].forEach(function (l) { const x = l[0], y = l[1]; rect(x, y - 8, 1, 8, '#5a3a22'); rect(x - 1, y - 11, 3, 3, '#ffd37c'); ctx.globalAlpha = 0.25; for (let d = 0; d < 5; d++) for (let dx = -d; dx <= d; dx++) px(x + dx, y - 10 - (5 - d), '#ffb347'); ctx.globalAlpha = 1; });
    rect(sx - 2, 194, 26, 3, '#b8ae9f'); rect(sx - 2, 197, 26, 2, '#8b8177');
    for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) { const bx2 = sx + i * 9; for (let dy = 0; dy < 4; dy++) { const hw = 3 - dy; rect(bx2 + 3 - hw, 199 + dy, hw * 2, 1, '#6d655c'); } }
    rect(120, 188, 9, 16, '#e8dcb5'); rect(119, 185, 11, 3, '#a8443a'); px(124, 184, '#6b2a24');
    const wcx = 124, wcy = 186;
    for (let i = 1; i <= 8; i++) { px(wcx, wcy - i, '#fbf9f2'); px(wcx, wcy + i, '#fbf9f2'); px(wcx - i, wcy, '#fbf9f2'); px(wcx + i, wcy, '#fbf9f2'); }
    [[0, -1], [0, 1], [-1, 0], [1, 0]].forEach(function (d) { for (let i = 4; i <= 8; i++) px(wcx + d[0] * i + (d[1] ? 1 : 0), wcy + d[1] * i + (d[0] ? 1 : 0), '#e6d2e4'); });
    px(wcx, wcy, '#2b2a3a');
    [[70, 74], [92, 66], [110, 80], [300, 60], [318, 70], [58, 58], [132, 54], [246, 72], [268, 50]].forEach(function (b) { const x = b[0], y = b[1]; rect(x, y, 3, 1, '#2b2a3a'); rect(x + 3, y - 1, 2, 1, '#2b2a3a'); rect(x + 5, y, 3, 1, '#2b2a3a'); });
    ctx.globalAlpha = 0.16;
    for (let a = 0; a < 7; a++) { const th = -Math.PI + a / 6 * Math.PI; for (let r = 30; r < 170; r += 2) px((sx + Math.cos(th) * r) | 0, (sy + Math.sin(th) * r * 0.6) | 0, '#ffe7a3'); }
    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
    rect(0, 240, 384, 48, '#7b7168'); rect(0, 240, 384, 3, '#a49a8e');
    for (let y = 243; y < 288; y += 12) { rect(0, y, 384, 1, '#5c554d'); for (let x = (((y / 12) | 0) & 1) * 12; x < 384; x += 24) rect(x, y, 1, 12, '#5c554d'); }
    for (let x = 0; x < 384; x += 30) { rect(x, 226, 20, 16, '#8b8177'); rect(x, 226, 20, 2, '#a49a8e'); rect(x + 18, 226, 2, 16, '#6d655c'); }
    [[210, 200], [240, 206], [278, 202]].forEach(function (b) { const x = b[0], y = b[1]; rect(x, y, 3, 1, '#2b2a3a'); rect(x + 3, y + 1, 2, 1, '#2b2a3a'); rect(x + 5, y, 3, 1, '#2b2a3a'); });
    // 旗
    const fx = 34, ftop = 140;
    const fm = {}, fset = function (x, y, c) { fm[x + ',' + y] = c; };
    for (let y = 0; y < 104; y++) fset(0, ftop + y, y < 3 ? '#a06a3a' : '#5a3a22');
    for (let y = 0; y < 104; y++) fset(1, ftop + y, '#8a6440');
    [-1, 0, 1, 2].forEach(function (dx) { fset(dx, ftop - 3, '#ffd37c'); fset(dx, ftop - 2, '#c2661f'); });
    fset(0, ftop - 6, '#ffd37c'); fset(1, ftop - 6, '#ffd37c'); fset(0, ftop - 5, '#ffe7a3'); fset(1, ftop - 5, '#c2661f');
    const fw = 60;
    for (let x = 0; x < fw; x++) {
      const tt = x / fw;
      const top = ftop + 4 + Math.round(Math.sin(tt * Math.PI * 1.6) * 6);
      const h = 40 + Math.round(Math.sin(tt * Math.PI * 2.2 + 1) * 7);
      const swallow = x > fw - 14 ? Math.round((x - (fw - 14)) * 1.1) : 0;
      const fold = Math.sin(tt * Math.PI * 6);
      const base = fold > 0.4 ? '#e2584c' : fold < -0.4 ? '#a83226' : '#c9402f';
      for (let y = 0; y < h; y++) {
        const yy = top + y;
        if (swallow && (y > h / 2 - swallow && y < h / 2 + swallow)) continue;
        const edge = y < 2 || y > h - 3;
        fset(2 + x, yy, edge ? '#ffd37c' : base);
      }
      if (x % 9 === 0) for (let y = 3; y < h - 3; y += 2) fset(2 + x, top + y, '#8a2a1e');
    }
    const crx = 18, cry = 14;
    const crown = ['..K.....K.....K..', '.KYK...KYK...KYK.', '.KYK...KYK...KYK.', 'KYYYK.KYYYK.KYYYK', 'KYYYYKYYYYYKYYYYK', 'KYYYYYYYYYYYYYYYK', 'KYYYYYYYYYYYYYYYK', 'KGGGGGGGGGGGGGGGK', '.KKKKKKKKKKKKKKK.'];
    const cpal = { Y: '#ffd37c', G: '#c2661f', K: '#8a4a10' };
    crown.forEach(function (row, y) { row.split('').forEach(function (ch, x) { if (cpal[ch]) fset(2 + crx + x, ftop + cry + y, cpal[ch]); }); });
    [2, 8, 14].forEach(function (dx) { fset(2 + crx + dx, ftop + cry, '#ffe7a3'); });
    [[14, 24], [30, 24]].forEach(function (q) {
      for (let y = 0; y < 12; y++) for (let x = 0; x < 13; x++) {
        const edge = y === 0 || x === 0 || y === 11 || x === 12;
        fset(2 + q[0] + x, ftop + q[1] + y, edge ? '#ffd37c' : '#c9402f');
      }
    });
    const fhas = function (x, y) { return !!fm[x + ',' + y]; };
    Object.keys(fm).forEach(function (k) { const xy = k.split(',').map(Number); px(fx + xy[0], xy[1], fm[k]); });
    for (let y = ftop - 8; y < ftop + 108; y++) for (let x = -2; x < 60; x++) if (!fhas(x, y) && (fhas(x - 1, y) || fhas(x + 1, y) || fhas(x, y - 1) || fhas(x, y + 1))) px(fx + x, y, '#1e1d2a');
    // はかり師（両手を上げて よろこぶ・後ろ姿）
    const hero = ['SS.........SS', 'SS.........SS', 'cc..HHHHH..cc', '.cc.HhhhH..cc', '.cc.HhhhH.cc.', '..cc.HHH..cc.', '..ccWWWWWcc..', '..CCCCCCCCC..', '..CCCCCCCCC..', '..CCCCCCCCC..', '..CC.....CC..', '..LL.....LL..', '..KKK...KKK..'];
    const hp = { H: '#4a2c1a', h: '#8a5a30', W: '#fbf9f2', C: '#3a5fb0', c: '#3a5fb0', B: '#2f3f75', L: '#6b4a2e', K: '#2b2a3a', S: '#f1cfa5' };
    pixelsOf(hero, hp).out.forEach(function (p) { const s = 3; for (let dy = 0; dy < s; dy++) for (let dx = 0; dx < s; dx++) px(150 + p[0] * s + dx, 196 + p[1] * s + dy, p[2]); });
    for (let i = 0; i < 60; i++) { const x = rnd() * 384 | 0, y = rnd() * 240 | 0, cs = ['#ffd37c', '#e2584c', '#6cbd6f', '#5d8fd8', '#fbf9f2']; rect(x, y, 3, 4, cs[i % 5]); }
  }

  // ---- 地下（古い巨石の壁）----
  function dungeon(ctx) {
    const t = tools(ctx), px = t.px, rect = t.rect;
    const rnd = makeRnd(404);
    rect(0, 0, W, H, '#25324a');
    for (let yy = 0; yy < 200; yy++) {
      const row = (yy / 20) | 0, off = (row & 1) * 16;
      for (let xx = 0; xx < W; xx++) {
        const bx = ((xx + off) / 32) | 0;
        const v = (Math.imul(bx * 197 + row * 89, 2654435761) >>> 0) % 11;
        let c = v < 3 ? '#2c3a54' : v < 6 ? '#26334b' : '#212c42';
        if (yy % 20 === 19 || ((xx + off) % 32) === 0) c = '#18202f';
        if (yy % 20 === 0) c = '#3a4a68';
        px(xx, yy, c);
      }
    }
    for (let i = 0; i < 7; i++) {
      let x = rnd() * W | 0, y = rnd() * 180 | 0;
      for (let k = 0; k < 26 + (rnd() * 30 | 0); k++) { px(x, y, '#12131c'); px(x + 1, y, '#1b1d28'); x += rnd() < 0.5 ? 1 : (rnd() < 0.5 ? -1 : 0); y += rnd() < 0.7 ? 1 : 0; }
    }
    for (let i = 0; i < 700; i++) { const x = rnd() * W | 0, y = 40 + (rnd() * 170 | 0); if (y % 20 > 15 || rnd() < 0.25) px(x, y, rnd() < 0.5 ? '#2f5a44' : '#26483a'); }
    for (let i = 0; i < 9; i++) {
      let x = 10 + (rnd() * 364 | 0), y = 0; const len = 24 + (rnd() * 60 | 0);
      for (let k = 0; k < len; k++) { px(x, y, '#2a3a2c'); px(x + 1, y, '#1f2c22'); if (rnd() < 0.3) x += rnd() < 0.5 ? 1 : -1; y++; }
      for (let k = 0; k < 5; k++) px(x + (rnd() * 3 | 0) - 1, y + (rnd() * 4 | 0), '#3f6a4a');
    }
    for (let i = 0; i < 26; i++) {
      const x = 14 + (rnd() * 356 | 0), y = 18 + (rnd() * 160 | 0);
      for (let k = 0; k < 4; k++) { px(x + k, y, '#191a24'); px(x + k, y + 5, '#191a24'); }
      px(x, y + 1, '#191a24'); px(x + 3, y + 2, '#191a24'); px(x + 1, y + 3, '#191a24');
    }
    rect(0, 200, W, 88, '#1d2739'); rect(0, 200, W, 2, '#141c2a');
    for (let y = 202; y < 288; y += 6) rect(0, y, W, 1, '#243044');
    rect(0, 236, W, 52, '#1a2740');
    for (let i = 0; i < 260; i++) { const x = rnd() * W | 0, y = 238 + (rnd() * 48 | 0); rect(x, y, 2 + (rnd() * 4 | 0), 1, rnd() < 0.3 ? '#2a4a5e' : '#16202f'); }
    for (let y = 240; y < 286; y += 3) { const w = 10 + (y - 240) * 0.7; for (let x = 160 - w / 2; x < 160 + w / 2; x++) if (rnd() < 0.4) px(x | 0, y, rnd() < 0.5 ? '#c8863a' : '#8a5a2a'); }
    const brazier = function (x, y) {
      rect(x - 6, y + 10, 13, 4, '#2b2d3c'); rect(x - 4, y + 4, 9, 7, '#3a3c4c'); rect(x - 4, y + 4, 9, 1, '#4a4c60');
      rect(x - 3, y, 7, 4, '#3fa9c8'); rect(x - 2, y - 3, 5, 3, '#7fd6e8'); px(x, y - 5, '#d8f4ff'); px(x - 1, y - 4, '#a8e6f4');
      ctx.globalAlpha = 0.18; for (let d = 0; d < 16; d++) for (let dx = -d; dx <= d; dx += 2) px(x + dx, y - 16 + d, '#3fa9c8'); ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
    };
    const torch = function (x, y) {
      for (let r = 46; r > 0; r -= 2) {
        ctx.globalAlpha = 0.2 * (1 - r / 50);
        for (let dy = -r; dy <= r; dy++) {
          const hw = Math.round(Math.sqrt(Math.max(0, r * r - dy * dy)));
          for (let dx = -hw; dx <= hw; dx++) if (r > 26 ? ((x + dx + y + dy) & 1) === 0 : true) px(x + dx, y + dy, r > 30 ? '#6a4a28' : r > 20 ? '#8a5a2a' : r > 12 ? '#c8863a' : '#ffb347');
        }
      }
      ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
      rect(x - 2, y + 4, 5, 3, '#3a3c4c'); rect(x - 1, y + 6, 3, 9, '#5a3a22'); px(x, y + 14, '#3b2616');
      rect(x - 2, y, 5, 5, '#e8842a'); rect(x - 1, y - 3, 3, 3, '#f4a23c'); rect(x - 1, y + 1, 3, 3, '#ffd37c');
      px(x, y - 5, '#ffe7a3'); px(x, y - 1, '#fff3c4'); px(x - 3, y + 2, '#c2661f'); px(x + 3, y + 2, '#c2661f');
    };
    torch(13, 72); torch(371, 72); torch(13, 148); torch(371, 148);
    brazier(13, 224); brazier(371, 224);
    for (let i = 0; i < 260; i++) { const side = rnd() < 0.5, x = side ? (rnd() * 26 | 0) : 358 + (rnd() * 26 | 0), y = rnd() * 240 | 0; px(x, y, rnd() < 0.5 ? '#2f5a44' : '#26483a'); }
    for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) {
      let x = rnd() < 0.5 ? 4 + (rnd() * 20 | 0) : 360 + (rnd() * 20 | 0), y = 0; const len = 40 + (rnd() * 70 | 0);
      for (let k = 0; k < len; k++) { px(x, y, '#2a3a2c'); px(x + 1, y, '#1f2c22'); if (rnd() < 0.28) x += rnd() < 0.5 ? 1 : -1; y++; }
      for (let k = 0; k < 6; k++) px(x + (rnd() * 3 | 0) - 1, y + (rnd() * 5 | 0), '#3f6a4a');
    }
    rect(150, 36, 84, 18, '#141c2a'); rect(146, 33, 92, 4, '#2b3a54'); rect(146, 33, 92, 1, '#3a4a68');
    for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) rect(150, 38 + i * 4, 84, 1, '#18202f');
    for (let i = 0; i < 30; i++) px(152 + (rnd() * 80 | 0), 38 + (rnd() * 14 | 0), '#1d2739');
    torch(126, 44); torch(258, 44);
  }

  // ---- 地下クリア（宝の間）----
  function dungeonClear(ctx) {
    const t = tools(ctx), px = t.px, rect = t.rect;
    const rnd = makeRnd(6502);
    rect(0, 0, W, H, '#111b34');
    for (let i = 0; i < 1800; i++) { const x = rnd() * W | 0, y = rnd() * H | 0; px(x, y, rnd() < 0.5 ? '#16233f' : '#0e1730'); }
    const demon = ['.D..........D.', '.DD........DD.', '.DDD..DD..DDD.', '..DDDDDDDDDD..', '..DDDDDDDDDD..', '..DDRRDDRRDD..', '..DDDDDDDDDD..', '...DDDDDDDD...', '....DDDDDD....'];
    demon.forEach(function (row, y) { row.split('').forEach(function (ch, x) {
      if (ch === '.') return;
      const s = 12, c = ch === 'R' ? '#e0367f' : '#1a2748';
      for (let dy = 0; dy < s; dy++) for (let dx = 0; dx < s; dx++) px(120 + x * s + dx, 4 + y * s + dy, c);
    }); });
    for (let x = 0; x < W; x += 6) {
      const h = 14 + (rnd() * 30 | 0);
      for (let y = 0; y < h; y++) { const w = Math.max(1, Math.round((1 - y / h) * 5)); rect(x, y, w, 1, y > h - 4 ? '#16233f' : '#1d2c50'); }
    }
    for (let x = 0; x < 84; x++) for (let y = 0; y < H; y++) {
      const d = (84 - x) / 84;
      if (rnd() < d * 0.9) px(x, y, rnd() < 0.25 ? '#2f5a86' : rnd() < 0.5 ? '#1d3a5e' : '#16283f');
    }
    for (let i = 0; i < 26; i++) { const x = 4 + (rnd() * 74 | 0), y = 60 + (rnd() * 200 | 0), h = 10 + (rnd() * 22 | 0); for (let k = 0; k < h; k++) { const w = Math.max(1, Math.round((1 - k / h) * 5)); rect(x, y + k, w, 1, k < h * 0.4 ? '#6fb6e0' : '#3f7fb0'); } }
    for (let x = 300; x < W; x++) for (let y = 0; y < H; y++) { const d = (x - 300) / 84; if (rnd() < d * 0.9) px(x, y, rnd() < 0.4 ? '#24374f' : '#18263c'); }
    [96, 288].forEach(function (pcx) {
      rect(pcx - 11, 30, 22, 258, '#3a4a66'); rect(pcx - 11, 30, 3, 258, '#4c6183'); rect(pcx + 7, 30, 4, 258, '#26344d');
      for (let y = 36; y < 288; y += 18) { rect(pcx - 13, y, 26, 4, '#42557a'); rect(pcx - 13, y, 26, 1, '#5b7299'); }
      rect(pcx - 15, 22, 30, 9, '#4c6183'); rect(pcx - 15, 22, 30, 2, '#6a82aa');
    });
    rect(310, 74, 62, 150, '#0c1226');
    for (let a = 0; a <= 72; a++) { const th = Math.PI + a / 72 * Math.PI; const x = 341 + Math.cos(th) * 31, y = 104 + Math.sin(th) * 31; if (y <= 104) rect(x | 0, y | 0, 2, 2, '#4c6183'); }
    rect(316, 82, 50, 142, '#17305c');
    for (let i = 0; i < 50; i++) px(318 + (rnd() * 46 | 0), 84 + (rnd() * 60 | 0), '#e6e0d2');
    for (let dy = -5; dy <= 5; dy++) { const hw = Math.round(Math.sqrt(Math.max(0, 25 - dy * dy))); rect(340 - hw, 100 + dy, hw * 2, 1, '#fff3c4'); }
    for (let i = 0; i < 8; i++) { const x = 318 + i * 6, h = 12 + (rnd() * 10 | 0); for (let k = 0; k < h; k++) { const w = Math.max(1, Math.round((1 - k / h) * 6)); rect(x, 150 - k, w, 1, '#1d3a24'); } }
    for (let i = 0; i < 7; i++) { const y = 224 - i * 12, w = 44 - i * 3, x = 316 + i * 3; rect(x, y, w, 12, i % 2 ? '#3a4a66' : '#32405c'); rect(x, y, w, 2, '#4c6183'); }
    for (let i = 0; i < 12; i++) {
      const w = 92 + i * 15, x = 192 - w / 2, y = 164 + i * 8;
      rect(x, y, w, 8, i % 2 ? '#41557c' : '#384a6e'); rect(x, y, w, 2, '#56709c'); rect(x, y + 7, w, 1, '#22304c');
      for (let bx = x + 8; bx < x + w; bx += 20) rect(bx, y + 2, 2, 5, '#2a3a5c');
    }
    const ccx = 192, ccy = 112;
    ctx.globalAlpha = 0.22;
    for (let r = 86; r > 0; r -= 2) for (let dy = -r; dy <= r; dy++) { const hw = Math.round(Math.sqrt(Math.max(0, r * r - dy * dy))); for (let dx = -hw; dx <= hw; dx++) if (r > 44 ? ((ccx + dx + ccy + dy) & 1) === 0 : true) px(ccx + dx, ccy + dy - 8, r > 62 ? '#7a5424' : r > 36 ? '#c8863a' : '#ffd37c'); }
    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
    ctx.globalAlpha = 0.2;
    for (let a = 0; a < 16; a++) { const th = a / 16 * Math.PI * 2; for (let r = 20; r < 120; r += 2) px((ccx + Math.cos(th) * r) | 0, (ccy - 8 + Math.sin(th) * r * 0.8) | 0, a % 2 ? '#ffd37c' : '#ffe7a3'); }
    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
    rect(ccx - 36, ccy - 50, 72, 24, '#8a5a30'); rect(ccx - 36, ccy - 50, 72, 3, '#a97440'); rect(ccx - 36, ccy - 29, 72, 3, '#5a3a22');
    rect(ccx - 38, ccy - 50, 5, 24, '#ffd37c'); rect(ccx + 33, ccy - 50, 5, 24, '#c2661f');
    rect(ccx - 30, ccy - 44, 60, 14, '#c9402f'); rect(ccx - 30, ccy - 44, 60, 3, '#e2584c');
    rect(ccx - 38, ccy - 26, 76, 34, '#7a4a2a'); rect(ccx - 38, ccy - 26, 76, 3, '#a06a3a'); rect(ccx - 38, ccy + 5, 76, 3, '#4a2c18');
    rect(ccx - 30, ccy - 20, 60, 22, '#c9402f'); rect(ccx - 30, ccy - 20, 60, 3, '#e2584c');
    [ccy - 22, ccy - 4].forEach(function (y) { rect(ccx - 40, y, 80, 5, '#ffd37c'); rect(ccx - 40, y + 4, 80, 1, '#c2661f'); });
    rect(ccx - 7, ccy - 14, 14, 16, '#ffd37c'); rect(ccx - 3, ccy - 9, 6, 8, '#3b2616');
    for (let i = 0; i < 60; i++) { const x = ccx - 30 + (rnd() * 60 | 0), y = ccy - 30 + (rnd() * 12 | 0); rect(x, y, 4, 3, '#ffd37c'); px(x + 1, y, '#ffe7a3'); }
    for (let i = 0; i < 30; i++) { const x = ccx - 50 + (rnd() * 100 | 0), y = ccy + 8 + (rnd() * 12 | 0); rect(x, y, 5, 3, '#ffd37c'); px(x + 4, y + 2, '#c2661f'); }
    const torch = function (x, y) {
      for (let r = 30; r > 0; r -= 2) {
        ctx.globalAlpha = 0.16 * (1 - r / 34);
        for (let dy = -r; dy <= r; dy++) { const hw = Math.round(Math.sqrt(Math.max(0, r * r - dy * dy))); for (let dx = -hw; dx <= hw; dx++) if (r > 20 ? ((x + dx + y + dy) & 1) === 0 : true) px(x + dx, y + dy, r > 24 ? '#6a4a28' : r > 14 ? '#8a5a2a' : r > 8 ? '#c8863a' : '#ffb347'); }
      }
      ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
      rect(x - 3, y + 4, 7, 4, '#2b3a52'); rect(x - 1, y + 8, 3, 26, '#2b3a52'); rect(x - 5, y + 33, 11, 4, '#26375c'); rect(x - 5, y + 33, 11, 1, '#3c5480');
      rect(x - 2, y, 5, 5, '#e8842a'); rect(x - 1, y - 4, 3, 4, '#f4a23c'); rect(x - 1, y + 1, 3, 3, '#ffd37c'); px(x, y - 6, '#ffe7a3');
    };
    torch(88, 150); torch(296, 150); torch(340, 176);
    const hp = { H: '#4a2c1a', h: '#8a5a30', S: '#f1cfa5', K: '#2b2a3a', W: '#fbf9f2', B: '#2f3f75', C: '#3a5fb0', c: '#6d8fd8', A: '#9aa3b8', a: '#c9d0dd', L: '#6b4a2e' };
    const hs = 3;
    pixelsOf(root.MT.Sprites.HERO_BACK, hp).out.forEach(function (p) { for (let dy = 0; dy < hs; dy++) for (let dx = 0; dx < hs; dx++) px(163 + p[0] * hs + dx, 118 + p[1] * hs + dy, p[2]); });
    for (let i = 0; i < 150; i++) { const x = 96 + (rnd() * 192 | 0), y = 40 + (rnd() * 170 | 0); px(x, y, rnd() < 0.5 ? '#ffe7a3' : '#ffd37c'); }
  }

  const SCENES = { room: room, explain: explain, redo: redo, result: result, home: home, roofChest: roofChest, roofView: roofView, dungeon: dungeon, dungeonClear: dungeonClear };

  function draw(canvas, name, opts) {
    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
    ctx.clearRect(0, 0, W, H);
    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
    ctx.__batch = null;
    try { (SCENES[name] || room)(ctx, opts || {}); } catch (e) { /* 絵が描けなくても 画面は使える */ }
    finally { if (ctx.__batch) ctx.__batch.flush(); ctx.__batch = null; ctx.globalAlpha = 1; }
  }

  root.MT = root.MT || {};
  root.MT.Scenes = { draw: draw, W: W, H: H, homePlaques: homePlaques, homeSign: homeSign };
})(typeof globalThis !== 'undefined' ? globalThis : this);


/* 面積の塔: 問題の板（方眼＋図形）
 * 板の実寸から 1マスの大きさを決め、方眼・図形・寸法・補助線を ひとつの SVG 座標にのせる（ずれない）。
 * - マスにタイルを おく（tiles）
 * - 指でなぞって 補助線を引く（直角に曲がる線も。図形の外にも のばせる）
 */
(function (root) {
  'use strict';
  const NS = 'http://www.w3.org/2000/svg';
  const INK = '#2b2a3a';
  const PAPER = '#f6efd9';
  const GRIDLINE = '#e8dcb5';
  const SHAPE = '#5d8fd8';
  const SHAPE_LINE = '#2f3f75';
  const DIM = '#c2661f';
  const HELP = '#7b60a9';
  const LIT = '#d8362a';   // 解説の「ここを見る」（赤い 太線）
  // 図の 外に 出した 寸法線は 1本ずつ 色を かえる（1本目は ふだんの 色）
  const DIM_COLORS = [DIM, '#2f8a57', '#8a4fc7', '#c23b6b', '#1f7f8c', '#8a6a12'];
  // 辺に そえる 小さい 寸法線（しるし）の 色。2つ 以上 あるときに 順に 使う
  // 外に 出した 寸法線の 色（DIM_COLORS）と かぶらない 色に する
  const MARK_COLORS = ['#3b5fc0', '#a02c8a', '#0f6b6b', '#7a4a21', '#5b6b12', '#4a5568'];
  const FREE_FILLS = ['#5d8fd8', '#6cbd6f', '#ef9448', '#a48ad0'];     // 自分で切ったとき（1つ目は そのまま青）
  const GUIDE_FILLS = ['#6cbd6f', '#ef9448', '#a48ad0', '#5d8fd8'];    // 解説（左=緑、右=オレンジ）
  const TILE = '#ffd37c';        // おいた タイル
  const TILE_LINE = '#b8862f';

  // はじめから おいてある タイルの マス（tilesPlaced: 上の rows だん / 左の cols れつ）
  function placedCells(spec) {
    const out = new Set();
    const placed = spec && spec.tilesPlaced;
    const F = root.MT.Figure;
    if (!placed || !spec.fig || !F) return out;
    const b = F.bounds(spec.fig);
    F.cellSet(spec.fig).forEach(function (k) {
      const c = F.parseKey(k);
      const rowOk = placed.rows != null && (c.y - b.y1) >= (b.h - placed.rows);
      const colOk = placed.cols != null && (c.x - b.x1) < placed.cols;
      if (rowOk || colOk) out.add(k);
    });
    return out;
  }

  function svg(tag, attrs, parent) {
    const e = document.createElementNS(NS, tag);
    Object.keys(attrs || {}).forEach(function (k) { if (attrs[k] != null) e.setAttribute(k, attrs[k]); });
    if (parent) parent.appendChild(e);
    return e;
  }
  function key(x, y) { return x + ',' + y; }

  function labelText(l, unit) {
    return l.text != null ? l.text : ((l.value != null ? l.value : (l.b - l.a)) + unit);
  }

  function create(host) {
    const Figure = root.MT.Figure;
    host.classList.add('board');
    const root_ = svg('svg', { class: 'board-svg', 'shape-rendering': 'crispEdges' });
    host.appendChild(root_);
    let model = {};
    let geo = null;
    let drag = null;
    let tileDrag = null;

    const ro = typeof ResizeObserver !== 'undefined' ? new ResizeObserver(function () { draw(); }) : null;
    if (ro) ro.observe(host);

    // 同じ大きさ・同じ中身なら 描きなおさない（画面を作るたびに 3回 描いていた。大きい図は 重い）
    let version = 0;
    let drawnKey = '';
    function set(m) { model = m || {}; version += 1; draw(); }

    // 図の 中を 通る 寸法線は 図の 外へ 出す（線と 数が 図と かさならないように。本人の指摘 2026-09-15）。外は 列を ずらして ならべる
    // 寸法線は ぜんぶ 図の 外に「列」で ならべる（辺の 数より 外。短いものほど 図の 近く。かさならない 列には いっしょに 入れる）
    function outsideDims(spec, b, margin, cellPx, fsPx) {
      const cp = cellPx || 10, fp = fsPx || 15;
      const all = (spec.labels || []).map(function (l) { return { l: l, hid: false }; }).concat((spec.hidden || []).map(function (l) { return { l: l, hid: true }; }));
      // 行ごと・列ごとの 図の はし（寸法線から 図の かどへ 引く 点線と、どちらの がわに 出すかに 使う）
      const maps = { rowMin: {}, rowMax: {}, colMin: {}, colMax: {} };
      Figure.cellSet(spec.fig).forEach(function (k) {
        const c = Figure.parseKey(k);
        if (maps.rowMin[c.y] == null || c.x < maps.rowMin[c.y]) maps.rowMin[c.y] = c.x;
        if (maps.rowMax[c.y] == null || c.x + 1 > maps.rowMax[c.y]) maps.rowMax[c.y] = c.x + 1;
        if (maps.colMin[c.x] == null || c.y < maps.colMin[c.x]) maps.colMin[c.x] = c.y;
        if (maps.colMax[c.x] == null || c.y + 1 > maps.colMax[c.x]) maps.colMax[c.x] = c.y + 1;
      });
      const origSide = function (l) {
        if (l.axis === 'x') return l.at <= b.y1 ? 'bottom' : l.at >= b.y2 ? 'top' : (l.at - b.y1 <= b.y2 - l.at ? 'bottom' : 'top');
        return l.at <= b.x1 ? 'left' : l.at >= b.x2 ? 'right' : (l.at - b.x1 <= b.x2 - l.at ? 'left' : 'right');
      };
      // 図が へこんでいる がわ（かいだんの 角が ある がわ）に 出すと、点線が 角に まっすぐ とどいて わかりやすい
      const sideFor = function (l) {
        const full = l.axis === 'x' ? (l.a <= b.x1 && l.b >= b.x2) : (l.a <= b.y1 && l.b >= b.y2);
        if (full) return origSide(l);
        let lo = 0, hi = 0;
        [l.a, l.b - 1].forEach(function (e) {
          if (l.axis === 'y') { if (maps.rowMin[e] != null) { lo = Math.max(lo, maps.rowMin[e] - b.x1); hi = Math.max(hi, b.x2 - maps.rowMax[e]); } }
          else if (maps.colMin[e] != null) { lo = Math.max(lo, maps.colMin[e] - b.y1); hi = Math.max(hi, b.y2 - maps.colMax[e]); }
        });
        if (hi > lo) return l.axis === 'y' ? 'right' : 'top';
        if (lo > hi) return l.axis === 'y' ? 'left' : 'bottom';
        return origSide(l);
      };
      const sides = { bottom: [], top: [], left: [], right: [] };
      const sideOf = new Map();
      all.forEach(function (o, i) {
        const l = o.l;
        if (l.kind !== 'dim' || l.at == null) return;
        // 出さない「?」の 寸法線に 列を とらない（列だらけで 図が 小さく なって いた）
        if (o.hid) {
          const same = function (d) { return d && d.axis === l.axis && d.a === l.a && d.b === l.b; };
          const used = (model.litDims || []).some(same) || (model.helpSegs || []).some(function (grp) { return grp.some(same); });
          if (!used) return;
        }
        // 塔: 各階の よこは 帯の 中に 書く（外に 積むと 図が つぶれる）。まどの 寸法は ふつうに 外へ
        if (spec.dimsInside && l.axis === 'x' && String(l.facing || '').indexOf('floor') === 0) return;
        const side = sideFor(l);
        sideOf.set(o, side);
        sides[side].push({ o: o, len: l.b - l.a, i: i });
      });
      const newAt = new Map();
      Object.keys(sides).forEach(function (side) {
        const vertical = side === 'left' || side === 'right';
        // 1列目は 辺の 数の 外、2列目からは 字の 高さ（＋すきま）ごと
        const first = vertical ? margin + (fp * 1.3 + 22) / cp : margin + (fp * 1.3 + 14) / cp;
        const gap = vertical ? (fp * 0.62 * 4 + 20) / cp : (fp * 1.3 + 12) / cp;
        const lanes = [];   // lanes[k] = その列に 入っている はんい
        sides[side].sort(function (p, q) { return (p.len - q.len) || (p.i - q.i); }).forEach(function (it) {
          const l = it.o.l;
          const pad = vertical ? (fp * 1.3 + 6) / cp : (fp * 0.62 * 4 + 12) / cp;   // 数の はばの ぶん あける
          let k = 0;
          while (lanes[k] && lanes[k].some(function (r) { return l.a < r.b + pad && l.b > r.a - pad; })) k += 1;
          (lanes[k] = lanes[k] || []).push({ a: l.a, b: l.b });
          const d = first + k * gap;
          newAt.set(it.o, side === 'bottom' ? b.y1 - d : side === 'top' ? b.y2 + d : side === 'left' ? b.x1 - d : b.x2 + d);
        });
      });
      const out = { labels: [], hidden: [], maps: maps };
      all.forEach(function (o) {
        const l = newAt.has(o) ? Object.assign({}, o.l, { at: newAt.get(o), at0: o.l.at, side: sideOf.get(o) }) : o.l;
        (o.hid ? out.hidden : out.labels).push(l);
      });
      return out;
    }

    // 寸法線の 両はしから 図の はし（かど）まで 細い 点線（どこから どこまでの 長さか わかるように）
    // 線と 同じ色で 引き、図の かどに 点を 打つ（線が まじわっても、どの かどまでの 長さか 色で たどれる）
    function drawExt(pl, l, g, color) {
      const m = pl.ext && pl.ext.maps;
      if (!m || !l.side) return;
      const col = color || '#c9a37a';
      [l.a, l.b].forEach(function (end, i) {
        const idx = i === 0 ? l.a : l.b - 1;
        let fx, fy, tx, ty;
        if (l.axis === 'y') {
          let ex = l.side === 'right' ? m.rowMax[idx] : m.rowMin[idx];
          if (ex == null) ex = l.side === 'right' ? pl.ext.b.x2 : pl.ext.b.x1;
          fx = pl.X(ex); fy = pl.Y(end); tx = pl.X(l.at); ty = fy;
        } else {
          let ey = l.side === 'top' ? m.colMax[idx] : m.colMin[idx];
          if (ey == null) ey = l.side === 'top' ? pl.ext.b.y2 : pl.ext.b.y1;
          fx = pl.X(end); fy = pl.Y(ey); tx = fx; ty = pl.Y(l.at);
        }
        svg('line', { x1: fx, y1: fy, x2: tx, y2: ty, stroke: col, 'stroke-width': 2, 'stroke-dasharray': '5 4', opacity: 0.75 }, g);
        svg('circle', { cx: fx, cy: fy, r: Math.max(4, Math.min(7, geo.cell * 0.22)), fill: col, stroke: '#fbf9f2', 'stroke-width': 1.5 }, g);
        (pl.extDots = pl.extDots || []).push({ x: fx, y: fy });
      });
    }

    function extentOf(spec, margin, marginX, cellPx, fsPx) {
      const b = Figure.bounds(spec.fig);
      const moved = outsideDims(spec, b, margin, cellPx, fsPx);
      const mx = Math.max(margin, marginX || 0);
      let x1 = b.x1 - mx, x2 = b.x2 + mx, y1 = b.y1 - margin, y2 = b.y2 + margin;
      moved.labels.concat(moved.hidden).forEach(function (l) {
        if (l.kind !== 'dim' || l.at == null) return;
        if (l.axis === 'x') { y1 = Math.min(y1, l.at - margin * 0.9); y2 = Math.max(y2, l.at + margin * 0.9); }
        else { x1 = Math.min(x1, l.at - margin * 1.2); x2 = Math.max(x2, l.at + margin * 1.2); }
      });
      if (spec.caption || (spec.marks || []).some(function (m) { return m.kind === 'perimeter'; })) y1 -= margin * 0.9;
      if (spec.areaText) y2 += margin * 1.25;
      if ((spec.rectMarks || []).some(function (m) { return !m.inside; })) y2 += margin * 0.9;
      return { x1: x1, x2: x2, y1: y1, y2: y2, w: x2 - x1, h: y2 - y1, b: b, labels: moved.labels, hidden: moved.hidden, maps: moved.maps };
    }

    // 数や「?」の 置き場所が かさならないように（あとから 置くものを 辺から はなれる ほうへ ずらす）
    let occupied = [];
    function boxOf(pt, w, hh) {
      const x = pt.anchor === 'middle' ? pt.x - w / 2 : pt.anchor === 'start' ? pt.x : pt.x - w;
      return { x1: x, y1: pt.y - hh / 2, x2: x + w, y2: pt.y + hh / 2 };
    }
    function hits(bx) { return occupied.some(function (o) { return bx.x1 < o.x2 && bx.x2 > o.x1 && bx.y1 < o.y2 && bx.y2 > o.y1; }); }
    function reserve(pt, w, hh) { occupied.push(boxOf(pt, w, hh)); return pt; }
    // 板の 中に おさめる（ふちで 切れないように）
    function clampIn(p, w, hh) {
      const bx = boxOf(p, w, hh);
      let dx = 0, dy = 0;
      if (bx.x1 < 3) dx = 3 - bx.x1; else if (bx.x2 > geo.bw - 3) dx = geo.bw - 3 - bx.x2;
      if (bx.y1 < 3) dy = 3 - bx.y1; else if (bx.y2 > geo.bh - 3) dy = geo.bh - 3 - bx.y2;
      return { x: p.x + dx, y: p.y + dy, anchor: p.anchor };
    }
    // 辺の 数: かさなったら 辺に そって ずらす → だめなら 少しだけ 外へ（遠くへは とばさない）
    function overlapArea(bx) {
      let s = 0;
      occupied.forEach(function (o) {
        const w = Math.min(bx.x2, o.x2) - Math.max(bx.x1, o.x1), hh = Math.min(bx.y2, o.y2) - Math.max(bx.y1, o.y1);
        if (w > 0 && hh > 0) s += w * hh;
      });
      return s;
    }
    function placeFree(pt, w, hh, l) {
      const outDx = l.axis === 'x' ? 0 : (l.facing === 'right' ? 1 : -1) * (w * 0.35 + 4);
      const outDy = l.axis === 'x' ? (l.facing === 'up' ? -1 : 1) * hh * 0.8 : 0;
      const sx = l.axis === 'x' ? w * 0.55 + 4 : 0, sy = l.axis === 'x' ? 0 : hh * 0.95;
      // [辺に そって, 外へ] の ずらし方を 近い じゅんに ためし、かさならない 最初の 場所（なければ いちばん かさなりの 少ない 場所）
      const moves = [[0, 0], [1, 0], [-1, 0], [0, 1], [1, 1], [-1, 1], [2, 0], [-2, 0], [0, 2]];
      let best = null, bestScore = Infinity;
      for (let i = 0; i < moves.length; i += 1) {
        const m = moves[i];
        const c = clampIn({ x: pt.x + sx * m[0] + outDx * m[1], y: pt.y + sy * m[0] + outDy * m[1], anchor: pt.anchor }, w, hh);
        const score = overlapArea(boxOf(c, w, hh));
        if (score === 0) { best = c; break; }
        if (score < bestScore) { bestScore = score; best = c; }
      }
      return reserve(best || pt, w, hh);
    }

    function layout(bw, bh) {
      const figs = model.figures || [];
      const n = figs.length;
      const vert = model.stack === 'v' && n > 1;
      // 名前の ふだ（左・右 など）の 帯。図の 大きさに よらず 同じ 高さを とる
      const nameBand = model.names ? Math.max(40, Math.min(58, bh * 0.11)) : 0;
      const gap = n > 1 ? 1.2 : 0;
      let margin = 1.4, marginX = 1.4, cell = 10, exts = null;
      // 左・右に 書く 数の いちばん 長い 字数
      let sideChars = 0;
      figs.forEach(function (f) {
        const unit = f.unit || (f.fig && f.fig.unit) || 'cm';
        (f.labels || []).forEach(function (l) { if (l.axis === 'y' && !(l.kind === 'dim' && l.at != null)) sideChars = Math.max(sideChars, String(labelText(l, unit)).length); });
      });
      const sizeOf = function () {
        if (vert) return { w: Math.max.apply(null, exts.map(function (e) { return e.w; })), h: exts.reduce(function (s, e) { return s + e.h; }, 0), band: nameBand * n };
        return { w: exts.reduce(function (s, e) { return s + e.w; }, 0) + gap * (n - 1), h: Math.max.apply(null, exts.map(function (e) { return e.h; })), band: nameBand };
      };
      let fsGuess = 15;
      for (let pass = 0; pass < 6; pass += 1) {
        exts = figs.map(function (f) { return extentOf(f, margin, marginX, cell, fsGuess); });
        const sz = sizeOf();
        cell = Math.min(bw / sz.w, Math.max(40, bh - sz.band) / sz.h);
        const cap = Math.min(bw, bh) / (model.bigCap ? 3 : 4.4);
        cell = Math.min(cell, cap, 92);
        if (cell >= 6) cell = Math.floor(cell);
        const fsPx = labelFont(cell, bh);
        const fsChanged = fsPx !== fsGuess;
        fsGuess = fsPx;
        const need = (fsPx * 1.3 + 24) / cell;                                   // 上・下: 字の 高さ＋辺との すきま（しるしつきの ぶんも）
        const needX = sideChars ? (sideChars * fsPx * 0.62 + 8 + 22) / cell : 0;   // 左・右: 字の はば＋すきま
        if (need <= margin + 0.05 && needX <= marginX + 0.05 && !fsChanged && pass > 0) break;
        // あきは px の 必要量から 毎回 きめなおす（大きく なる 一方だと、図が 小さく なる ほど あきが ふくらむ）
        margin = Math.min(Math.max(1.0, need), 12);
        marginX = Math.min(Math.max(margin, needX), 14);
      }
      const sz = sizeOf();
      const mk = function (e, ox, oy) {
        return {
          ext: e,
          X: function (x) { return ox + (x - e.x1) * cell; },
          Y: function (y) { return oy + (e.y2 - y) * cell; },
          left: ox, top: oy, right: ox + e.w * cell, bottom: oy + e.h * cell
        };
      };
      let placed;
      if (vert) {
        let cy = (bh - sz.h * cell - sz.band) / 2;
        placed = exts.map(function (e) { const pl = mk(e, (bw - e.w * cell) / 2, cy); cy += e.h * cell + nameBand; return pl; });
      } else {
        const oy0 = (bh - sz.h * cell - sz.band) / 2;
        let cursor = (bw - sz.w * cell) / 2;
        placed = exts.map(function (e) { const pl = mk(e, cursor, oy0 + (sz.h - e.h) / 2 * cell); cursor += (e.w + gap) * cell; return pl; });
      }
      // 方眼の ある 図を 2つ 以上 ならべるときは、2つめ からの 図の かどを 1つめの 方眼に のせる
      if (n > 1 && figs.every(function (f) { return f.grid !== false; })) {
        const p0 = placed[0], b0 = p0.ext.b;
        placed = placed.map(function (pl, i) {
          if (!i) return pl;
          const bi = pl.ext.b;
          const dx = (pl.X(bi.x1) - p0.X(b0.x1)) / cell, dy = (pl.Y(bi.y2) - p0.Y(b0.y2)) / cell;
          const sx = (Math.ceil(dx - 1e-6) - dx) * cell, sy = (Math.round(dy) - dy) * cell;
          return mk(pl.ext, pl.left + sx, pl.top + sy);
        });
      }
      return { cell: cell, placed: placed, margin: margin, fs: labelFont(cell, bh), bw: bw, bh: bh, nameBand: nameBand };
    }

    function labelFont(cell, bh) {
      const vhMax = Math.max(14, Math.min(36, (root.innerHeight || 700) * 0.034));
      const byCell = cell * 0.92;
      return Math.max(15, Math.min(vhMax, Math.max(byCell, 15)));
    }

    function draw() {
      const bw = host.clientWidth, bh = host.clientHeight;
      const k = bw + 'x' + bh + ':' + version;
      if (bw && bh && k === drawnKey && root_.firstChild) return;
      while (root_.firstChild) root_.removeChild(root_.firstChild);
      if (!bw || !bh) { drawnKey = ''; return; }
      drawnKey = k;
      occupied = [];
      host.__boardAudit = [];
      root_.setAttribute('width', bw);
      root_.setAttribute('height', bh);
      root_.setAttribute('viewBox', '0 0 ' + bw + ' ' + bh);
      defs();
      if (model.tilingDemo) { drawTiling(bw, bh); return; }
      const figs = model.figures || [];
      if (!figs.length) { geo = null; return; }
      geo = layout(bw, bh);
      host.__geo = { cell: geo.cell, margin: geo.margin, fs: geo.fs, ext: geo.placed.map(function (p) { return { w: p.ext.w, h: p.ext.h, x1: p.ext.x1, x2: p.ext.x2, y1: p.ext.y1, y2: p.ext.y2, dims: p.ext.labels.concat(p.ext.hidden).filter(function (l) { return l.kind === 'dim'; }).map(function (l) { return l.axis + ':' + l.a + '-' + l.b + '@' + l.at + ' ' + (l.side || '-'); }) }; }) };   // 点検用
      const gridOn = figs.every(function (f) { return f.grid !== false; }) && geo.cell >= 8 && model.innerGrid !== false;
      if (gridOn) drawBoardGrid(bw, bh, geo.placed[0], geo.cell);
      figs.forEach(function (spec, i) { drawFigure(spec, geo.placed[i], i); });
      if (model.names) {
        figs.forEach(function (spec, i) {
          const pl = geo.placed[i];
          const b = pl.ext.b;
          const cx = (pl.X(b.x1) + pl.X(b.x2)) / 2;
          tag(cx, pl.bottom + geo.nameBand / 2, model.names[i], { fs: Math.max(18, Math.min(geo.fs * 1.1, 28)), bg: model.nameColors && model.nameColors[i] ? model.nameColors[i] : '#fbf9f2', fg: model.nameColors && model.nameColors[i] ? '#fbf9f2' : INK });
        });
      }
      drawLines();
      bindInteraction();
    }

    function defs() {
      const d = svg('defs', {}, root_);
      const pat = svg('pattern', { id: 'hatch', width: 12, height: 12, patternUnits: 'userSpaceOnUse', patternTransform: 'rotate(45)' }, d);
      svg('rect', { width: 12, height: 12, fill: PAPER }, pat);
      svg('rect', { width: 6, height: 12, fill: '#e2e0d4' }, pat);
    }

    function drawBoardGrid(bw, bh, pl, cell) {
      const g = svg('g', { class: 'board-grid' }, root_);
      const x0 = pl.X(Math.floor(pl.ext.x1));
      const y0 = pl.Y(Math.ceil(pl.ext.y2));
      const startX = x0 - Math.ceil(x0 / cell) * cell;
      const startY = y0 - Math.ceil(y0 / cell) * cell;
      for (let x = startX; x <= bw + cell; x += cell) svg('line', { x1: x, y1: 0, x2: x, y2: bh, stroke: GRIDLINE, 'stroke-width': 2 }, g);
      for (let y = startY; y <= bh + cell; y += cell) svg('line', { x1: 0, y1: y, x2: bw, y2: y, stroke: GRIDLINE, 'stroke-width': 2 }, g);
    }

    function partsCells(spec, cells) {
      // 色分け: 解説・ガイドの parts があればそれ、なければ 自分で引いた線で分かれた かたまり
      const map = {};
      if (model.parts && model.parts.length) {
        let fillIndex = 0;
        model.parts.forEach(function (p, i) {
          if (p.role === 'box' || p.role === 'cutout') return;
          const color = (model.partColors || GUIDE_FILLS)[fillIndex % 4];
          fillIndex += 1;
          for (let x = p.x; x < p.x + p.w; x += 1) for (let y = p.y; y < p.y + p.h; y += 1) map[key(x, y)] = { color: color, part: i };
        });
        return map;
      }
      const lines = model.lines || [];
      if (!lines.length) return map;
      const blocked = new Set();
      lines.forEach(function (l) {
        if (l.x1 === l.x2) { for (let y = Math.min(l.y1, l.y2); y < Math.max(l.y1, l.y2); y += 1) blocked.add(key(l.x1 - 1, y) + '|' + key(l.x1, y)); }
        else if (l.y1 === l.y2) { for (let x = Math.min(l.x1, l.x2); x < Math.max(l.x1, l.x2); x += 1) blocked.add(key(x, l.y1 - 1) + '|' + key(x, l.y1)); }
      });
      const comps = Figure.components(cells, blocked);
      if (comps.length < 2) return map;
      comps.forEach(function (comp, i) { comp.forEach(function (k) { map[k] = { color: FREE_FILLS[i % 4], part: i }; }); });
      return map;
    }

    function drawFigure(spec, pl, figIndex) {
      const fig = spec.fig;
      const cell = geo.cell;
      const cells = Figure.cellSet(fig);
      const b = Figure.bounds(fig);
      const g = svg('g', { class: 'board-fig' }, root_);
      const primary = figIndex === 0;
      const colored = primary ? partsCells(spec, cells) : {};
      const placed = spec.tilesPlaced || null;
      const pre = placed ? placedCells(spec) : null;
      const tiles = primary && model.tiles ? model.tiles : null;
      const miss = primary && model.missCells ? model.missCells : null;
      const baseColor = (model.figColors && model.figColors[figIndex]) || SHAPE;
      // タイルを おく問題は、形を ぬらずに あき地（紙の色）にして、タイルだけを タイルらしく 見せる（本人の指摘 2026-09-15）
      const tileMode = spec.interaction === 'tiles' || !!placed;
      // 重ねた2つの形は、べつべつの すける色で（同じ色だと 1つに見えてしまう）
      const overlay = !!(spec.pieceColors && fig.pieces && fig.pieces.length > 1);
      // 囲んでひく（box/cutout）
      if (primary && model.parts) {
        model.parts.forEach(function (p) {
          if (p.role === 'cutout') svg('rect', { x: pl.X(p.x), y: pl.Y(p.y + p.h), width: p.w * cell, height: p.h * cell, fill: 'url(#hatch)', stroke: '#c9402f', 'stroke-width': 3, 'stroke-dasharray': '8 5' }, g);
        });
      }
      // 図形の 中の 方眼は「マスを 数えて よい 問題」だけ（本人の指定 2026-09-15: 背景の 方眼は あってよい）
      const gridIn = fig.grid !== false && cell >= 6 && model.innerGrid !== false;
      cells.forEach(function (k) {
        const c = Figure.parseKey(k);
        let fill = baseColor;
        if (colored[k]) fill = colored[k].color;
        if (tileMode || overlay) fill = '#fbf9f2';
        if (miss && miss.has(k)) fill = '#ef9448';
        const r = svg('rect', { x: pl.X(c.x), y: pl.Y(c.y + 1), width: cell, height: cell, fill: fill, class: 'fig-cell' }, g);
        if (gridIn) { r.setAttribute('stroke', tileMode ? '#b9b09a' : SHAPE_LINE); r.setAttribute('stroke-width', 1); }
        r.dataset.cell = k;
        r.dataset.fig = figIndex;
        if ((pre && pre.has(k)) || (tiles && tiles.has(k))) {
          const inset = Math.max(1, Math.min(3, cell * 0.08));
          svg('rect', { x: pl.X(c.x) + inset, y: pl.Y(c.y + 1) + inset, width: cell - inset * 2, height: cell - inset * 2, fill: TILE, stroke: TILE_LINE, 'stroke-width': 2, 'pointer-events': 'none', class: 'fig-tile' }, g);
        }
      });
      // 正方形パズル: 中の 区切り（どこが 1つの 正方形か）を 描く。これが ないと 解けない
      if (spec.pieceEdges && fig.pieces) fig.pieces.forEach(function (pc) {
        if (pc.role === 'hole') return;
        svg('rect', { x: pl.X(pc.x), y: pl.Y(pc.y + pc.h), width: pc.w * cell, height: pc.h * cell, fill: 'none', stroke: INK, 'stroke-width': 2, 'pointer-events': 'none' }, g);
      });
      if (overlay) {
        fig.pieces.forEach(function (pc, i) {
          const attrs = { x: pl.X(pc.x), y: pl.Y(pc.y + pc.h), width: pc.w * cell, height: pc.h * cell, fill: spec.pieceColors[i % spec.pieceColors.length], 'fill-opacity': 0.6, stroke: i ? '#c9402f' : '#2f5fa8', 'stroke-width': 4, 'pointer-events': 'none' };
          if (i) attrs['stroke-dasharray'] = '12 6';
          svg('rect', attrs, g);
        });
        fig.pieces.forEach(function (pc, i) {
          if (pc.name) tag(pl.X(pc.x + pc.w) - geo.fs * 1.1, pl.Y(pc.y + pc.h) + geo.fs * 0.95, pc.name, { fs: Math.max(14, Math.min(geo.fs, 24)), bg: i ? '#c9402f' : '#2f5fa8', fg: '#fbf9f2' });
        });
      }
      // 長方形を 色の わくと 名前で 見せる（がくぶちの 外がわ・内がわ。答えの 欄と 同じ 色）
      if (primary) (spec.rectMarks || []).forEach(function (m) {
        if (m.key && model.activeMark !== m.key) return;   // いま 入れている 欄の ものだけ
        // あなまで うめて 見せる（外がわの 長方形は 中まで つまった 形）
        if (m.fill) {
          svg('rect', { x: pl.X(m.fill.x), y: pl.Y(m.fill.y + m.fill.h), width: m.fill.w * cell, height: m.fill.h * cell, fill: SHAPE, 'fill-opacity': 0.55, 'pointer-events': 'none' }, g);
          if (m.fillText) {
            const fz2 = Math.max(14, Math.min(geo.fs * 0.9, 22));
            const fcx = pl.X(m.fill.x + m.fill.w / 2), fcy = pl.Y(m.fill.y + m.fill.h / 2);
            tag(fcx, fcy, m.fillText, { fs: fz2, bg: '#fbf9f2', fg: m.color });
            const tw2 = String(m.fillText).length * fz2 * 0.92 + 20;
            occupied.push({ x1: fcx - tw2 / 2, x2: fcx + tw2 / 2, y1: fcy - fz2 * 0.75, y2: fcy + fz2 * 0.75 });
          }
        }
        const pad = m.inside ? 5 : -5;
        const x = pl.X(m.x) + pad, y = pl.Y(m.y + m.h) + pad, w = m.w * cell - pad * 2, hh = m.h * cell - pad * 2;
        svg('rect', { x: x, y: y, width: w, height: hh, fill: 'none', stroke: m.color, 'stroke-width': 4, 'stroke-dasharray': '10 6', 'pointer-events': 'none' }, g);
        const fz = Math.max(15, Math.min(geo.fs, 24));
        const tw = String(m.text).length * fz * 0.92 + 20, th = fz * 1.5;
        const cx = m.inside ? x + w / 2 : x + tw / 2;
        const cy = m.inside ? y + hh / 2 : y - th / 2 - 6;
        tag(cx, cy, m.text, { fs: fz, bg: m.color, fg: '#fbf9f2' });
        occupied.push({ x1: cx - tw / 2, x2: cx + tw / 2, y1: cy - th / 2, y2: cy + th / 2 });
      });
      // 囲む大きい長方形（点線）
      if (primary && model.parts) {
        model.parts.forEach(function (p) {
          if (p.role === 'box') svg('rect', { x: pl.X(p.x), y: pl.Y(p.y + p.h), width: p.w * cell, height: p.h * cell, fill: 'none', stroke: '#3b7dd8', 'stroke-width': 3, 'stroke-dasharray': '10 6' }, g);
        });
      }
      // 外わく（太線）。重ねた形は それぞれの わくを 上で 描いたので いらない
      if (!overlay) Figure.boundaryEdgesOfCells(cells).forEach(function (e) {
        if (e.axis === 'h') svg('line', { x1: pl.X(e.from), y1: pl.Y(e.at), x2: pl.X(e.to), y2: pl.Y(e.at), stroke: INK, 'stroke-width': 3, 'stroke-linecap': 'square' }, g);
        else svg('line', { x1: pl.X(e.at), y1: pl.Y(e.from), x2: pl.X(e.at), y2: pl.Y(e.to), stroke: INK, 'stroke-width': 3, 'stroke-linecap': 'square' }, g);
      });
      // 解説で「面積」の らんを うめるとき: 形の ふちを 赤い 太線で かこむ（どこの 面積かを 見せる）
      if (primary && model.litArea && !overlay) Figure.boundaryEdgesOfCells(cells).forEach(function (e) {
        if (e.axis === 'h') svg('line', { x1: pl.X(e.from), y1: pl.Y(e.at), x2: pl.X(e.to), y2: pl.Y(e.at), stroke: LIT, 'stroke-width': 7, 'stroke-linecap': 'square' }, g);
        else svg('line', { x1: pl.X(e.at), y1: pl.Y(e.from), x2: pl.X(e.at), y2: pl.Y(e.to), stroke: LIT, 'stroke-width': 7, 'stroke-linecap': 'square' }, g);
      });
      // 解説の 行（外・内・かこむ など）が 図の どの長方形かを、行と 同じ色の わくで 見せる
      if (primary && model.litRect) {
        const lr = model.litRect;
        svg('rect', { x: pl.X(lr.x), y: pl.Y(lr.y + lr.h), width: lr.w * cell, height: lr.h * cell, fill: 'none', stroke: model.litRectColor || LIT, 'stroke-width': 6, 'stroke-dasharray': '10 6' }, g);
      }
      // 問題が 名ざしする 辺を 赤い 太線で（「赤い 辺は…」。本人の指定 2026-09-17）
      if (primary && spec.redEdges) spec.redEdges.forEach(function (d) {
        if (d.axis === 'x') svg('line', { x1: pl.X(d.a), y1: pl.Y(d.at), x2: pl.X(d.b), y2: pl.Y(d.at), stroke: LIT, 'stroke-width': 9, 'stroke-linecap': 'round' }, g);
        else svg('line', { x1: pl.X(d.at), y1: pl.Y(d.a), x2: pl.X(d.at), y2: pl.Y(d.b), stroke: LIT, 'stroke-width': 9, 'stroke-linecap': 'round' }, g);
      });
      // 「ここを見る」の 辺に ラベルが ないとき（切った長方形の たて・よこ など）も 赤い 太線を 引く
      if (primary) (model.litDims || []).forEach(function (d) {
        if (!d || d.at == null) return;
        if (d.axis === 'x') svg('line', { x1: pl.X(d.a), y1: pl.Y(d.at), x2: pl.X(d.b), y2: pl.Y(d.at), stroke: LIT, 'stroke-width': 9, 'stroke-linecap': 'round' }, g);
        else svg('line', { x1: pl.X(d.at), y1: pl.Y(d.a), x2: pl.X(d.at), y2: pl.Y(d.b), stroke: LIT, 'stroke-width': 9, 'stroke-linecap': 'round' }, g);
      });
      // いま出している長方形（やり直し・ガイド）: 太いわく
      if (primary && model.activePart != null && model.parts && model.parts[model.activePart]) {
        const p = model.parts[model.activePart];
        svg('rect', { x: pl.X(p.x) + 2, y: pl.Y(p.y + p.h) + 2, width: p.w * cell - 4, height: p.h * cell - 4, fill: 'none', stroke: '#ffd37c', 'stroke-width': 5 }, g);
        svg('rect', { x: pl.X(p.x), y: pl.Y(p.y + p.h), width: p.w * cell, height: p.h * cell, fill: 'none', stroke: INK, 'stroke-width': 3 }, g);
      }
      // しるし
      (spec.marks || []).forEach(function (m) {
        if (m.kind === 'diagonal') {
          svg('line', { x1: pl.X(b.x1), y1: pl.Y(b.y1), x2: pl.X(b.x2), y2: pl.Y(b.y2), stroke: '#c9402f', 'stroke-width': 3, 'stroke-dasharray': '8 6' }, g);
          if (m.text) tag((pl.X(b.x1) + pl.X(b.x2)) / 2, (pl.Y(b.y1) + pl.Y(b.y2)) / 2, m.text, { fs: geo.fs * 0.85, fg: '#c9402f' });
        } else if (m.kind === 'perimeter') {
          tag((pl.X(b.x1) + pl.X(b.x2)) / 2, pl.bottom - geo.margin * geo.cell * 0.45, m.text, { fs: Math.max(13, Math.min(geo.fs * 0.85, 24)) });
        } else if (m.kind === 'enclose-hint') {
          svg('rect', { x: pl.X(b.x1), y: pl.Y(b.y2), width: b.w * cell, height: b.h * cell, fill: 'none', stroke: '#3b7dd8', 'stroke-width': 3, 'stroke-dasharray': '10 6' }, g);
        }
      });
      // 寸法・辺の長さ
