            h('div', { class: 'pic' }, [grid(c.fill)]),
            h('div', { class: 'tx', text: c.text })
          ]);
        })),
        h('div', { class: 'how-rules' }, rules.map(function (r) {
          return h('div', { class: 'how-rule' + (r.cls ? ' ' + r.cls : '') }, [
            r.icon ? h('div', { class: 'ic' }, [r.icon, h('span', { class: 'gate-tag', plain: '関' })]) : h('span', { class: 'c', style: 'background:' + r.color }),
            h('div', { class: 'bd' }, [h('div', { class: 'tt', text: r.title }), h('div', { class: 'tx', text: r.text })])
          ]);
        })),
        h('button', { class: 'answer-btn how-go', type: 'button', onclick: function () { m.close(); } }, [UI.tx(onDone ? '分かった！ 始める　▶' : 'とじる')])
      ]
    });
  }

  // ---- 先生の 番号（管理者画面の 入り口）----
  // 1回 入れた 端末は 管理者モードの まま（読みこみなおしても 聞かない）。3回 ちがうと とじる
  const ADMIN_CODE = '7351';
  function askAdminCode(ctx) {
    let tries = 0;
    UI.modal({
      title: '先生の 番号',
      body: function (body, close) {
        const input = h('input', { class: 'px-input', type: 'password', inputmode: 'numeric', maxlength: 8, autocomplete: 'off', 'aria-label': '番号' });
        const msg = h('div', { class: 'modal-msg' });
        const go = function () {
          if (String(input.value).trim() === ADMIN_CODE) { close(); if (UI.adminOn) UI.adminOn(); ctx.go('admin'); return; }
          tries += 1;
          input.value = '';
          if (tries >= 3) { close(); return; }
          msg.textContent = '番号が ちがう';
          input.focus();
        };
        input.addEventListener('keydown', function (ev) { if (ev.key === 'Enter' && !ev.isComposing) go(); });
        body.appendChild(h('p', { class: 'how-note', text: '先生用の 画面です。番号を 入れてください。' }));
        body.appendChild(input);
        body.appendChild(h('button', { class: 'answer-btn', type: 'button', onclick: go }, ['開く']));
        body.appendChild(msg);
        setTimeout(function () { input.focus(); }, 30);
      }
    });
  }

  // ---- なまえ ----
  function openName(ctx) {
    const E = ctx.engine;
    UI.modal({
      title: '名前',
      body: function (body, close) {
        const input = h('input', { class: 'px-input', type: 'text', maxlength: 20, placeholder: '名前', autocomplete: 'off', 'aria-label': '名前' });
        const msg = h('div', { class: 'modal-msg' });
        const go = function () {
          if (!E.setName(input.value)) { msg.textContent = '名前を 書いてね。'; input.focus(); return; }
          close();
          startGame(ctx);
        };
        input.addEventListener('keydown', function (ev) { if (ev.key === 'Enter' && !ev.isComposing) go(); });
        body.appendChild(h('p', { class: 'how-note', text: '君の 名前を 書いてね。' }));
        body.appendChild(input);
        body.appendChild(h('button', { class: 'answer-btn', type: 'button', onclick: go }, ['始める']));
        body.appendChild(msg);
        const codeIn = h('input', { class: 'px-input', type: 'text', placeholder: 'ひきつぎコード', autocomplete: 'off', 'aria-label': 'ひきつぎコード' });
        const codeMsg = h('div', { class: 'modal-msg' });
        body.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'set-sep' }));
        body.appendChild(h('p', { class: 'how-note', text: '別の たんまつの 続きから（ひきつぎコード）' }));
        body.appendChild(codeIn);
        body.appendChild(h('button', { class: 'opt-btn', type: 'button', onclick: function () {
          try { E.importCode(codeIn.value); close(); ctx.render(); } catch (err) { codeMsg.textContent = err.message || 'コードが ちがうみたい。'; }
        } }, ['続きから']));
        body.appendChild(codeMsg);
        setTimeout(function () { input.focus(); }, 30);
      }
    });
  }

  // ---- 手帳 ----
  function openHandbook(ctx) {
    const hb = ctx.engine.handbook();
    const list = h('ul', { class: 'hb-list' });
    hb.lines.forEach(function (l) { list.appendChild(h('li', { text: l.floor + '階：' + l.text })); });
    hb.blanks.forEach(function (b) { list.appendChild(h('li', { class: 'blank', text: b.floor + '階：' + b.text })); });
    UI.modal({ title: 'はかり手の 手帳', wide: true, body: [list] });
  }

  UI.openHandbook = openHandbook;
  UI.openHow = openHow;
  UI.startGame = startGame;
})(typeof globalThis !== 'undefined' ? globalThis : this);


/* 面積の塔: 問題の画面（地上の10問・ずかんの再戦・地下で共通）
 * 左: 番人＋ふきだし（問題）＋方眼の板 / 右: 答えの入力。上: 階と 石畳の道
 * 答えたら はんこと帯を出し、「つぎへ」で次の問題へ（エンジンは もう進んでいる）
 */
(function (root) {
  'use strict';
  const MT = root.MT;
  const UI = MT.UI;
  const h = UI.h;
  const Guide = MT.Guide;
  UI.screens = UI.screens || {};

  // ---- 共通の小さな関数（解説・やり直しでも使う） ----
  function formulaText(q) {
    const steps = (q.explain && q.explain.steps) || [];
    const lines = steps.filter(function (s) { return !s.kind && Array.isArray(s.parts); }).map(function (s) {
      return s.parts.map(function (p) { return typeof p === 'string' ? p : (p.answer + (p.unit || '')); }).join(' ').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
    });
    return lines.join('　');
  }

  // 図形の 中に 方眼を 引くのは、マスを 数える 問題（タイルを おく・4階まで）だけ。公式で とく 図は 中を ぬりつぶす
  function countable(q) {
    if (q.countable) return true;   // マスの 話を する 問題（5階・9階の 関所）
    if (q.figure && q.figure.interaction === 'tiles') return true;
    let fl = 99;
    try { fl = MT.Bank.get(q.templateId).floor; } catch (e) { /* 地下など */ }
    return fl <= 4;
  }

  function figuresModel(q) {
    if (q.tilingDemo) return { tilingDemo: true };
    if (q.figures && q.figures.length) {
      const n = q.figures.length;
      // noNames: えらぶ ものが 図の 名前では ないとき（名前の ふだは いらない）。stack: 'v' で たてに ならべる
      return { figures: q.figures, innerGrid: countable(q), stack: q.stack || null, names: q.noNames ? null : n === 2 ? ['左', '右'] : n === 3 ? ['左', 'まん中', '右'] : q.figures.map(function (f, i) { return String(i + 1); }) };
    }
    if (q.figure) return { figures: [q.figure], innerGrid: countable(q) };
    return null;
  }

  function mountBoard(wrap, model, noteText, dark) {
    if (!model) { wrap.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'board-note' + (dark ? ' dark' : '') }, [UI.txPhrases(noteText || '')])); return null; }
    const host = h('div', { class: 'board' });
    wrap.appendChild(host);
    const b = MT.Board.create(host);
    b.set(model);
    UI.onMounted(function () { b.draw(); });
    requestAnimationFrame(function () { b.draw(); });
    return b;
  }

  function floorLabelOf(sess) {
    if (!sess) return '';
    if (sess.floorNo >= 100) return '地下';
    if (sess.floorNo === 15) return '屋上';
    return sess.floorNo + '階';
  }

  function guardOfSession(sess) {
    if (sess.rematch) return sess.rematch;
    return MT.Guards.guardFor(sess.floorNo);
  }

  // 筆算のマス目
  function hsCell(text, cls) { return h('span', { class: 'hs-cell' + (cls ? ' ' + cls : ''), plain: text || '' }); }
  function hissanGrid(hp) {
    const H = MT.Hissan;
    const wrap = h('div', { class: 'hs hs-' + hp.op });
    if (hp.op === 'div') {
      const g = hp.grid;
      wrap.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'hs-row hs-quotient' }, [hsCell('', 'hs-divisor-pad')].concat(g.quotientCells.map(function (qc) {
        if (!qc) return hsCell('');
        const st = qc.stepKey ? H.stepState(hp, qc.stepKey) : 'done';
        return hsCell(st === 'done' ? qc.text : (st === 'active' ? '?' : ''), st);
      }))));
      wrap.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'hs-row hs-dividend' }, [h('span', { class: 'hs-cell hs-divisor', plain: g.divisor })].concat(g.dividendCells.map(function (d) { return hsCell(d, 'done'); }))));
      g.rows.forEach(function (row) {
        const st = H.stepState(hp, row.stepKey);
        const cells = new Array(hp.cols).fill('');
        for (let i = 0; i < row.text.length; i += 1) if (row.start + i >= 0 && row.start + i < hp.cols) cells[row.start + i] = row.text.charAt(i);
        wrap.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'hs-row' }, [hsCell('', 'hs-divisor-pad')].concat(cells.map(function (c) { return hsCell(st === 'done' ? c : (st === 'active' && c ? '?' : ''), st === 'active' && c ? 'active' : (st === 'done' && c ? 'done' : '')); }))));
        if (row.underline) {
          wrap.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'hs-row hs-divider' }, [hsCell('', 'hs-divisor-pad')].concat(cells.map(function (c, i) { return h('span', { class: 'hs-line' + (i >= row.start && i <= row.end ? ' on' : '') }); }))));
        }
      });
      return wrap;
    }
    hp.grid.forEach(function (row) {
      if (row.type === 'divider') { wrap.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'hs-row hs-divider' }, new Array(hp.cols).fill(0).map(function () { return h('span', { class: 'hs-line on' }); }))); return; }
      if (row.type === 'operand') {
        const cells = row.cells.slice();
        if (row.sign) cells[0] = row.sign;
        wrap.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'hs-row' }, cells.map(function (c) { return hsCell(c, c ? 'done' : ''); })));
        return;
      }
      if (row.type === 'product') {
        const cells = new Array(hp.cols).fill('');
        const states = new Array(hp.cols).fill('');
        const carries = new Array(hp.cols).fill('');
        row.digitSteps.forEach(function (ds) {
          const st = H.stepState(hp, ds.key);
          const idx = hp.cols - 1 - ds.place;
          if (st === 'done') {
            const w = ds.write;
            for (let k = 0; k < w.length; k += 1) { const ci = idx - (w.length - 1 - k); if (ci >= 0) { cells[ci] = w.charAt(k); states[ci] = 'done'; } }
            if (ds.carry && idx - 1 >= 0) carries[idx - 1] = ds.carry;
          } else if (st === 'active') { cells[idx] = '?'; states[idx] = 'active'; }
        });
        wrap.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'hs-row' }, cells.map(function (c, i) {
          const node = hsCell(c, states[i]);
          if (carries[i]) node.appendChild(h('i', { class: 'hs-carry', plain: String(carries[i]) }));
          return node;
        })));
        return;
      }
      const st = H.stepState(hp, row.stepKey);
      wrap.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'hs-row' }, row.cells.map(function (c) { return hsCell(st === 'done' ? c : (st === 'active' && c ? '?' : ''), st === 'active' && c ? 'active' : (st === 'done' && c ? 'done' : '')); })));
    });
    return wrap;
  }
  function hissanOverlay(hp) {
    return h('div', { class: 'hissan-wrap' }, [h('div', { class: 'hissan-title', text: hp.title }), hissanGrid(hp)]);
  }

  function flash(wrap, text) {
    if (!wrap) return;
    const old = wrap.querySelector('.board-msg');
    if (old) old.remove();
    const m = h('div', { class: 'board-msg', text: text });
    wrap.appendChild(m);
    setTimeout(function () { if (m.parentNode) m.parentNode.removeChild(m); }, 2200);
  }

  function sessionHelpSegments(q, g) {
    const step = g && Guide.currentStep(g);
    if (!step || !step.dim || !q.figure) return null;
    const hidden = (q.figure.hidden || []).filter(function (l) { return l.axis === step.dim.axis && l.a === step.dim.a && l.b === step.dim.b; })[0];
    if (!hidden) {
      const lab = (q.figure.labels || []).filter(function (l) { return l.axis === step.dim.axis && l.a === step.dim.a && l.b === step.dim.b; })[0];
      return lab ? [[lab]] : null;
    }
    const sol = MT.Deduce.solve(q.figure.fig, q.figure.labels);
    const gap = sol.gaps[step.dim.axis].filter(function (x) { return x.a >= step.dim.a && x.b <= step.dim.b && x.path; })[0];
    return [[hidden].concat(gap ? gap.path.map(function (p) { return p.label; }) : [])];
  }

  // 単位から「面積」か「長さ」かを 見わける（聞かれているのが どっちか わかりにくい。本人の指摘 2026-09-16）
  function unitKind(unit) {
    const u = String(unit || '');
    if (!u) return '';
    // a・ha も 面積の 単位（ふだが 出ていなかった。2026-09-27）
    if (u.indexOf('²') >= 0 || u === 'a' || u === 'ha') return 'area';
    if (/^(mm|cm|m|km)$/.test(u)) return 'len';
    return '';
  }
  function askText(unit) {
    const k = unitKind(unit);
    if (k === 'area') return '面積を 答える（' + unit + '）';
    if (k === 'len') return '長さを 答える（' + unit + '）';
    if (unit === 'まい') return 'まい数を 答える';
    return unit ? unit + ' で 答える' : '';
  }
  function askTag(unit) {
    return h('div', { class: 'ask-tag ' + unitKind(unit), text: askText(unit) });
  }

  function numDisplay(value, unit, opts) {
    const o = opts || {};
    const cls = 'val' + (value === '' ? ' empty' : '') + (o.state ? ' ' + o.state : '');
    const uk = unitKind(unit);
    return h('div', { class: 'num-display' + (o.dark ? ' dark' : '') }, [h('div', { class: cls, plain: value === '' ? '?' : value }), unit ? h('div', { class: 'unit' + (uk ? ' ' + uk : ''), plain: unit }) : null]);
  }

  // 選択肢が 入りきらないときは、すきまと 文字を 1段ずつ 小さくする
  function fitChoices(box) {
    if (!box.isConnected) return;
    ['many', 'tight'].forEach(function (c) { if (box.scrollHeight > box.clientHeight + 2) box.classList.add(c); });
  }

  function choiceList(choices, o) {
    const maxLen = Math.max.apply(null, choices.map(function (c) { return String(c.text).length; }));
    const box = h('div', { class: 'choices' + (choices.length >= 5 ? ' many' : '') + (maxLen > 24 ? ' long' : '') });
    choices.forEach(function (c, i) {
      let cls = 'choice-btn' + (o.dark ? ' dark' : '');
      if (o.held) {
        if (c.id === o.given) cls += o.correct ? ' right' : ' wrong';
      } else if (c.id === o.selected) cls += ' sel';
      box.appendChild(h('button', { class: cls, type: 'button', onclick: function () { if (!o.held) o.onPick(c.id); } }, [h('span', { class: 'num', plain: String(i + 1) }), h('span', { text: c.text })]));
    });
    UI.onMounted(function () { fitChoices(box); });
    requestAnimationFrame(function () { fitChoices(box); });
    return box;
  }

  function fieldRows(fields, values, focus, o) {
    const rows = h('div', { class: 'fill-rows' });
    fields.forEach(function (f) {
      const v = values[f.key] || '';
      let st = '';
      if (o.results && o.results[f.key]) st = o.results[f.key].correct ? ' ok' : ' ng';
      else if (focus === f.key) st = ' on';
      rows.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'fill-row' + (focus === f.key && !o.held ? ' active' : '') }, [
        h('span', { class: 'row-label', style: f.color ? 'color:' + f.color + ';font-weight:bold' : null }, f.color ? [h('i', { class: 'row-chip', style: 'background:' + f.color }), f.label] : [f.label]),
        h('button', { class: 'blank-btn' + st, type: 'button', onclick: function () { if (!o.held) o.onFocus(f.key); } }, [v === '' ? '□' : v]),
        h('span', { class: 'blank-unit', plain: f.unit || '' })
      ]));
    });
    return rows;
  }

  function orderList(items, order, o) {
    const names = {};
    items.forEach(function (i) { names[i.id] = i.text; });
    const list = h('div', { class: 'order-list' });
    order.forEach(function (id, idx) {
      list.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'order-item' }, [
        h('span', { class: 'n', plain: String(idx + 1) }),
        h('span', { class: 't', text: names[id] }),
        o.held ? null : h('button', { class: 'px-btn', type: 'button', 'aria-label': '上へ', onclick: function () { if (idx > 0) o.onSwap(idx, idx - 1); } }, ['↑']),
        o.held ? null : h('button', { class: 'px-btn', type: 'button', 'aria-label': '下へ', onclick: function () { if (idx < order.length - 1) o.onSwap(idx, idx + 1); } }, ['↓'])
      ]));
    });
    return list;
  }

  // ほかの子の 答案（大事な 問題なので ふきだしの 中ではなく 大きな カードで 見せる。本人の指摘 2026-09-15）
  function peerCard(q, dark) {
    if (!q || !q.peerAnswer) return null;
    return h('div', { class: 'peer-card' + (dark ? ' dark' : '') }, [h('span', { class: 'hd', text: '他の子の 答案' }), h('span', { class: 'tx', plain: q.peerAnswer })]);
  }

  Object.assign(UI, { peerCard: peerCard, formulaText: formulaText, figuresModel: figuresModel, mountBoard: mountBoard, hissanGrid: hissanGrid, hissanOverlay: hissanOverlay, flash: flash, numDisplay: numDisplay, choiceList: choiceList, fieldRows: fieldRows, orderList: orderList, floorLabelOf: floorLabelOf, guardOfSession: guardOfSession, unitKind: unitKind, askTag: askTag });

  function newState(key, q) {
    return {
      key: key,
      num: UI.numberInput(q.format === 'fields' ? 6 : 6),
      numState: '',
      choice: null,
      tiles: new Set(),
      order: q.order ? q.order.items.map(function (i) { return i.id; }) : null,
      pairs: [], pairA: '', pairB: '', pairFocus: 'a',
      fields: {}, fieldFocus: q.fields && q.fields.length ? q.fields[0].key : null,
      lines: [], hissan: null, help: null, hint: ''
    };
  }

  UI.screens.question = function (ctx) {
    const E = ctx.engine, st = ctx.state, p = E.progress();
    const hold = st.hold;
    const dungeonMain = hold ? hold.dungeonMain : E.phase() === 'underground-session';
    const sess = dungeonMain ? null : E.session();
    const q = hold ? hold.q : (dungeonMain ? E.undergroundQuestion() : E.currentQuestion());
    if (!q || (!dungeonMain && !sess)) { st.hold = null; E.goMap(); ctx.render(); return; }
    const ugs = p.undergroundSession;
    const ugNo = hold ? hold.ugNo : (ugs ? ugs.no : 1);
    const rematch = sess && sess.rematch ? sess.rematch : null;
    const dark = dungeonMain || !!(sess && sess.floorNo >= 100);
    const key = hold ? hold.key : (dungeonMain ? ['ug', ugs.no, ugs.stage, ugs.seed].join(':') : ['s', sess.startedAt, sess.index, q.templateId].join(':'));
    let qs = st.qs;
    if (!qs || qs.key !== key) qs = st.qs = newState(key, q);

    const guide = hold ? hold.guide : ((!dungeonMain && (q.format === 'cut' || q.format === 'line')) ? E.guide() : null);
    const gmode = guide ? guide.mode : null;
    const guided = gmode === 'full' || gmode === 'half' || gmode === 'three' || gmode === 'cutonly';
    const step = guided ? Guide.currentStep(guide) : null;
    const finalChoiceNow = guided && gmode === 'three' && guide.finished && q.finalChoice;
    const cutFree = q.figure && q.figure.interaction === 'cut';
    const drawable = !hold && !qs.hissan && !finalChoiceNow && (q.format === 'line' || (q.format === 'cut' && (!guided || (!step && !guide.finished))) || cutFree);

    let lines;
    if (hold) lines = hold.lines || [];
    else if (q.format === 'line') lines = dungeonMain ? (ugs ? ugs.lines : []) : (guide ? guide.lines : []);
    else if (guided) lines = guide.lines;
    else if (dungeonMain) lines = ugs ? ugs.lines : [];
    else lines = qs.lines;

    // ---- 背景 ----
    if (dark) UI.bg('dungeon', {}, { flicker: true, tintClass: 'dungeon-tint' });
    else UI.bg('room', { compound: q.format === 'cut' });
    // B30 では、つぎに出る 地下クリアの絵を 先に描いておく
    if (dungeonMain && ugNo >= 30) UI.prewarmScene('dungeonClear', {});

    const app = document.getElementById('app');
    const scr = h('div', { class: 'screen' });
    const layout = h('div', { class: 'q-layout' + (dark ? ' dungeon-layout' : '') });
    scr.appendChild(layout);

    // ---- 上の帯 ----
    const back = h('button', { class: 'back-btn' + (dark ? ' dark' : ''), type: 'button', 'aria-label': 'ホームへ', onclick: function () {
      st.hold = null;
      // 地下B30を とおった直後は、地下クリアの画面を 先に 見せる
      if (hold && hold.dungeonMain && hold.correct && hold.res && hold.res.finished) { ctx.render(); return; }
      E.goMap();
      ctx.render();
    } }, ['◀']);
    let headLeft;
    let roadEl, countText;
    if (dungeonMain) {
      headLeft = [back, h('div', { class: 'dungeon-depth', plain: '地下 B' + ugNo }), h('div', { class: 'dungeon-sub', text: '古の めいきゅう' })];
      const blockStart = Math.floor((ugNo - 1) / 10) * 10 + 1;
      const results = [];
      for (let d = blockStart; d < ugNo; d += 1) results.push(true);
      // 答えた直後は 旅人を いまの石に のこし、「おくへ」で 1マス 歩かせる
      if (hold && hold.correct && hold.res && hold.res.cleared) results.push(true);
      roadEl = UI.road(10, Math.min(9, ugNo - blockStart), results, null, { dark: true });
      countText = (ugNo - blockStart + 1) + '／10';
    } else {
      const status = E.sessionStatus();
      const label = floorLabelOf(sess);
      headLeft = [back, h('div', { class: dark ? 'dungeon-depth' : 'floor-label outline-text', plain: label })];
      if (rematch) headLeft.push(h('div', { class: 'head-tag', text: 'ずかんで 戦う' }));
      // 答えた直後は 旅人を 答えた石に のこし、「つぎへ」で 1マス 歩かせる
      const idx = hold && hold.sessIndex != null ? hold.sessIndex : Math.min(status.index, status.count - 1);
      roadEl = UI.road(status.count, idx, status.results, status.gates, { dark: dark });
      countText = (hold ? hold.sessIndex + 1 : status.index + 1) + '／' + status.count;
    }
    layout.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'q-head' }, [
      h('div', { class: 'q-head-left' }, headLeft),
      h('div', { class: 'q-head-right' }, [roadEl, h('div', { class: 'q-count' + (dark ? ' dark' : ''), plain: countText })])
    ]));

    // ---- 左: 番人・ふきだし・板 ----
    const body = h('div', { class: 'q-body' + (dark ? ' dungeon-body' : '') });
    layout.appendChild(body);
    const left = h('div', { class: 'q-left' });
    body.appendChild(left);
    const guardId = dungeonMain ? MT.Sprites.dungeonGuardFor(ugNo) : guardOfSession(sess);
    const tags = [];
    if (dungeonMain) {
      tags.push(h('span', { class: 'tag maze', text: 'めいきゅう' }));
      tags.push(h('span', { class: 'tag-note', text: 'とうたつ B' + Math.max(p.underground.reached, ugNo) + ' ／ B30' }));
    } else {
      if (q.role === 'surprise') { tags.push(h('span', { class: 'tag surprise', text: 'ぬきうち' })); tags.push(h('span', { class: 'tag-note', text: (q.originFloor || '') + '階の 番人の 関所。落とすと この階は やり直し' })); }
      else if (q.gate && !rematch) { tags.push(h('span', { class: 'tag', text: '関所' })); tags.push(h('span', { class: 'tag-note', text: '落とすと この階は やり直し' })); }
      if (q.role === 'carry') tags.push(h('span', { class: 'tag carry', text: 'もう一度' }));
      if (q.format === 'cut') tags.push(h('span', { class: 'tag kind', text: '組み合わせた形' }));
    }
    const bubbleKids = [];
    if (tags.length) bubbleKids.push(h('div', { class: 'tags' }, tags));
    bubbleKids.push(UI.txPhrases(q.prompt));
    let extraText = '';
    if (finalChoiceNow) extraText = q.finalChoice.prompt;
    else if (step) extraText = step.ask;
    else if (guided && gmode === 'three' && !guide.finished) {
      const rest = ((q.cut && q.cut.modes) || ['v', 'h', 'e']).filter(function (w) { return guide.waysDone.indexOf(w) < 0; });
      extraText = '残り：' + rest.map(Guide.wayName).join('、');
    }
    if (qs.hissan) extraText = '式は 合っている。筆算で たしかめよう。';
    if (extraText) bubbleKids.push(h('span', { class: 'step-ask', text: extraText }));
    if (qs.hint) bubbleKids.push(h('span', { class: 'step-ask say-help', text: qs.hint }));
    const bubble = h('div', { class: 'bubble' + (dark ? ' stone' : '') + UI.bubbleSizeClass(String(q.prompt) + extraText + qs.hint) }, bubbleKids);
    const speaker = [UI.guardBox(guardId, dark ? 'dark' : ''), bubble];
    // 線を けす・ヒント・手帳
    const canClear = !hold && (q.format === 'line' || q.format === 'cut' || cutFree) && lines.length > 0 && !(guided && step && guide.stepIndex > 0) && !finalChoiceNow;
    if (q.format === 'line' || q.format === 'cut' || cutFree) {
      speaker.push(h('button', { class: 'side-btn' + (canClear ? ' on' : ''), type: 'button', onclick: function () { if (canClear) clearLines(); } }, [UI.tx('線を 消す')]));
    }
    if (!hold && gmode === 'half' && !qs.hissan) {
      speaker.push(h('button', { class: 'side-btn on', type: 'button', onclick: function () { const hn = E.guideHint(); qs.hint = hn ? hn.text : ''; ctx.render(); } }, ['ヒント']));
    }
    if (q.handbook) speaker.push(h('button', { class: 'side-btn on', type: 'button', onclick: function () { UI.openHandbook(ctx); } }, [UI.tx('手帳')]));
    left.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'speaker-row' }, speaker));
    const pc = peerCard(q, dark);
    if (pc) left.appendChild(pc);

    const wrap = h('div', { class: 'board-wrap' + (dark ? ' dark' : '') + (hold ? (hold.correct ? ' ok' : ' ng') : '') });
    left.appendChild(wrap);
    const fm = figuresModel(q);
    let model = null;
    if (fm) {
      model = Object.assign({ showHidden: true, partColors: MT.Board.FREE_FILLS }, fm);
      if (!model.tilingDemo) {
        model.lines = lines;
        if (guided && guide.analysis && (guide.analysis.mode === 'cut' || guide.analysis.mode === 'enclose')) model.parts = Guide.partsForDisplay(guide);
        if (step && step.part != null) model.activePart = step.part;
        if (step && step.dim) model.litDims = [step.dim];
        if (q.format === 'fields') model.activeMark = hold ? null : qs.fieldFocus;
        if (qs.help) model.helpSegs = qs.help;
        const tilesQ = q.format === 'grid' && q.figure && q.figure.interaction === 'tiles';
        if (tilesQ) {
          const pre = MT.Board.placedCells(q.figure);
          model.tiles = qs.tiles;
          if (!hold) model.onTile = function (k, add) { if (pre.has(k)) return; if (add) qs.tiles.add(k); else qs.tiles.delete(k); board.set(model); drawRight(); };
        }
        if (drawable) model.onCut = onCut;
      }
    }
    // 図のない問題は 板に 問題文を大きく書く（答えたあとは はんこと重ならないよう 消す）
    const board = mountBoard(wrap, model, hold ? '' : (q.boardNote || q.prompt), dark);
    if (qs.hissan) wrap.appendChild(hissanOverlay(qs.hissan));
    if (hold) {
      UI.stamp(hold.correct, dark).forEach(function (n) { wrap.appendChild(n); });
      const title = hold.correct ? (dungeonMain ? '通った！' : 'せいかい！') : 'ちがう…';
      wrap.appendChild(UI.band(hold.correct, title, hold.fbText, { dark: dark }));
    }

    // ---- 右: 答え ----
    let right = buildRight();
    body.appendChild(right);
    function drawRight() { const n = buildRight(); right.replaceWith(n); right = n; }

    function buildRight() {
      const col = h('div', { class: 'q-right' });
      // 筆算（ガイドの計算で まちがえたとき）
      if (qs.hissan) {
        const cur = MT.Hissan.current(qs.hissan);
        col.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'col-label', text: cur ? (cur.label ? cur.label + '　' : '') + cur.expr + ' ＝ ?' : '' }));
        col.appendChild(numDisplay(qs.num.value, '', { dark: dark, state: qs.numState }));
        col.appendChild(UI.keypad(typeKey, { dark: dark }));
        col.appendChild(h('button', { class: 'answer-btn' + (dark ? ' dark' : '') + (qs.num.value ? '' : ' disabled'), type: 'button', onclick: answerHissan }, ['答える']));
        return col;
      }
      if (finalChoiceNow || q.format === 'choice') {
        const choices = finalChoiceNow ? q.finalChoice.choices : q.choices;
        col.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'col-label', text: '一つ 選ぼう' }));
        col.appendChild(choiceList(choices, { selected: qs.choice, held: !!hold, given: hold && hold.given, correct: hold && hold.correct, dark: dark, onPick: function (id) { qs.choice = id; drawRight(); } }));
        col.appendChild(answerButton(qs.choice != null, function () { submitAnswer(qs.choice); }));
        return col;
      }
      if (q.format === 'fields') {
        col.appendChild(fieldRows(q.fields, qs.fields, qs.fieldFocus, { held: !!hold, results: hold && hold.res && hold.res.result && hold.res.result.fields || (hold && hold.res && hold.res.fields) || null, onFocus: function (k) { focusField(k); } }));
        col.appendChild(UI.keypad(typeKey, { dark: dark }));
        const filled = q.fields.some(function (f) { return (qs.fields[f.key] || '') !== ''; });
        col.appendChild(answerButton(filled, submitFields));
        return col;
      }
      if (q.format === 'order') {
        col.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'col-label', text: '1が 一番 小さい' }));
        col.appendChild(orderList(q.order.items, qs.order, { held: !!hold, onSwap: function (a, b) { const t = qs.order[a]; qs.order[a] = qs.order[b]; qs.order[b] = t; drawRight(); } }));
        col.appendChild(answerButton(true, function () { submitAnswer(qs.order.slice()); }));
        return col;
      }
      if (q.format === 'pairs') {
        const row = h('div', { class: 'fill-row active' }, [
          h('span', { class: 'row-label', text: 'たて' }),
          h('button', { class: 'blank-btn' + (qs.pairFocus === 'a' ? ' on' : ''), type: 'button', onclick: function () { qs.pairFocus = 'a'; drawRight(); } }, [qs.pairA || '□']),
          h('span', { class: 'fill-part', plain: '×' }),
          h('span', { class: 'row-label', text: '横' }),
          h('button', { class: 'blank-btn' + (qs.pairFocus === 'b' ? ' on' : ''), type: 'button', onclick: function () { qs.pairFocus = 'b'; drawRight(); } }, [qs.pairB || '□']),
          h('button', { class: 'px-btn pair-add', type: 'button', onclick: addPair }, [UI.tx('入れる')])
        ]);
        col.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'fill-rows' }, [row]));
        col.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'pairs-found' }, qs.pairs.map(function (pr) { return h('span', { class: 'pair-chip', plain: pr[0] + '×' + pr[1] }); })));
        col.appendChild(UI.keypad(typeKey, { dark: dark }));
        col.appendChild(answerButton(qs.pairs.length > 0, function () { submitAnswer(qs.pairs.slice()); }, '全部 見つけた'));
        return col;
      }
      if (q.format === 'line') {
        col.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'col-label', text: '線を 引いたら' }));
        col.appendChild(h('div', { style: 'flex:1' }));
        col.appendChild(answerButton(lines.length > 0, function () { submitAnswer(null); }, 'これで 分ける'));
        return col;
      }
      if (guided && gmode === 'cutonly') {
        col.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'howto-card' }, [
          h('div', { class: 't', text: '線の 引き方' }),
          h('p', { text: '図の 上を なぞると、線が 引ける。' }),
          h('p', { text: '切り方は 3通り。どれでも いいよ。' }),
          h('ul', {}, [h('li', { text: 'たてに 切る' }), h('li', { text: '横に 切る' }), h('li', { text: 'まわりを かこんで、欠けた ところを ひく' })])
        ]));
        col.appendChild(h('div', { style: 'flex:1' }));
        // 答えたあとは「つぎへ」（数の 欄は 出さない）
        col.appendChild(hold ? answerButton(true, next) : h('button', { class: 'answer-btn wait', type: 'button' }, [UI.tx('長方形に 分けられたら 進む')]));
        return col;
      }
      if (guided && !step && !hold) {
        col.appendChild(numDisplay('', '', { dark: dark }));
        col.appendChild(UI.keypad(function () {}, { dark: dark }));
        col.appendChild(h('button', { class: 'answer-btn wait', type: 'button' }, [UI.tx('線を 引くと 進める')]));
        return col;
      }
      const tilesQ = q.format === 'grid' && q.figure && q.figure.interaction === 'tiles';
      // はじめから おいてある タイルも ふくめて 数える
      if (tilesQ) { const pre = MT.Board.placedCells(q.figure); let n = pre.size; qs.tiles.forEach(function (k) { if (!pre.has(k)) n += 1; }); col.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'col-label', text: '置いた タイル ' + n + ' まい' })); }
      else if (step) col.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'col-label', text: step.ask }));
      const unit = step ? step.unit : (q.unit || '');
      // 長さを 聞かれているのか 面積かを はっきり 出す
      if (!tilesQ && unitKind(unit)) col.appendChild(askTag(unit));
      const valState = hold ? (hold.correct ? 'ok' : 'ng') : qs.numState;
      col.appendChild(numDisplay(hold ? String(hold.given == null ? '' : hold.given) : qs.num.value, unit, { dark: dark, state: valState }));
      col.appendChild(UI.keypad(typeKey, { dark: dark }));
      col.appendChild(answerButton(qs.num.value !== '', function () { if (step) answerStep(); else submitAnswer(qs.num.value); }));
      return col;
    }

    function answerButton(ready, onGo, label) {
      if (hold) {
        let lab = '次へ　▶';
        let cls = 'answer-btn next';
        if (hold.dungeonMain) {
          if (hold.correct) lab = hold.res.extra ? '続きへ　▶' : (hold.res.finished ? '最後へ　▶' : 'おくへ 進む　▶');
          else { lab = 'もう一度'; cls = 'answer-btn'; }
          cls += ' dark';
        } else if (dark) cls += ' dark';
        return h('button', { class: cls, type: 'button', onclick: next }, [UI.tx(lab)]);
      }
      return h('button', { class: 'answer-btn' + (dark ? ' dark' : '') + (ready ? '' : ' disabled'), type: 'button', onclick: function () { if (ready) onGo(); } }, [UI.tx(label || (dungeonMain ? 'きざむ' : '答える'))]);
    }

    // 欄を えらぶ。図に その 欄の 長方形を 出す 問題（がくぶち）は 図も かきなおす
    function focusField(k) {
      qs.fieldFocus = k;
      if (board && model && q.figure && q.figure.rectMarks) { model.activeMark = k; board.set(model); }
      drawRight();
    }

    // ---- 入力 ----
    function typeKey(k) {
      if (hold) { if (k === 'enter') next(); return; }
      if (q.format === 'fields') {
        const f = qs.fieldFocus;
        if (k === 'enter') {
          const empty = q.fields.filter(function (x) { return (qs.fields[x.key] || '') === ''; })[0];
          if (empty && (qs.fields[f] || '') !== '') { focusField(empty.key); return; }
          if (q.fields.some(function (x) { return (qs.fields[x.key] || '') !== ''; })) submitFields();
          return;
        }
        let v = qs.fields[f] || '';
        if (k === 'back') v = v.slice(0, -1); else if (k === 'clear') v = ''; else if (v.length < 7) v = (v + k).replace(/^0+(?=\d)/, '');
        qs.fields[f] = v;
        drawRight();
        return;
      }
      if (q.format === 'pairs') {
        if (k === 'enter') { if (qs.pairA && !qs.pairB) { qs.pairFocus = 'b'; drawRight(); } else if (qs.pairA && qs.pairB) addPair(); return; }
        const fk = qs.pairFocus === 'a' ? 'pairA' : 'pairB';
        let v = qs[fk];
        if (k === 'back') v = v.slice(0, -1); else if (k === 'clear') v = ''; else if (v.length < 4) v = (v + k).replace(/^0+(?=\d)/, '');
        qs[fk] = v;
        drawRight();
        return;
      }
      if (k === 'enter') {
        if (!qs.num.value) return;
        if (qs.hissan) answerHissan(); else if (step) answerStep(); else submitAnswer(qs.num.value);
        return;
      }
      if (qs.numState === 'ng') { qs.num.clear(); qs.numState = ''; }
      if (k === 'back') qs.num.back(); else if (k === 'clear') qs.num.clear(); else qs.num.type(k);
      drawRight();
    }

    function addPair() {
      const x = MT.Util.parseInteger(qs.pairA), y = MT.Util.parseInteger(qs.pairB);
      if (x == null || y == null || x <= 0 || y <= 0) return;
      if (x * y !== q.pairs.area) { UI.se('wrong'); flash(wrap, x + '×' + y + '＝' + (x * y) + '。' + q.pairs.area + ' に ならない'); qs.pairA = ''; qs.pairB = ''; qs.pairFocus = 'a'; drawRight(); return; }
      const lo = Math.min(x, y), hi = Math.max(x, y);
      if (!qs.pairs.some(function (pr) { return pr[0] === lo && pr[1] === hi; })) qs.pairs.push([lo, hi]);
      UI.se('tap');
      qs.pairA = ''; qs.pairB = ''; qs.pairFocus = 'a';
      drawRight();
    }

    function submitFields() {
      const o = {};
      q.fields.forEach(function (f) { o[f.key] = qs.fields[f.key] || ''; });
      submitAnswer(o);
    }

    function answerStep() {
      if (!qs.num.value) return;
      const res = E.guideAnswer(qs.num.value);
      if (res.empty) return;
      if (!res.correct) {
        UI.se('wrong');
        if (res.calc && !qs.hissan) {
          qs.hissan = MT.Hissan.plan(res.calc[0], res.calc[1], res.calc[2]);
          qs.num.clear(); qs.numState = '';
          ctx.render();
          return;
        }
        if (res.stopCount >= 2) qs.help = sessionHelpSegments(q, guide);
        qs.numState = 'ng';
        ctx.render();
        return;
      }
      UI.se('tap');
      qs.num.clear(); qs.numState = ''; qs.hissan = null; qs.help = null; qs.hint = '';
      if (res.finished) { submitAnswer(String(res.total)); return; }
      ctx.render();
    }

    function answerHissan() {
      const hs = qs.hissan;
      const n = MT.Util.parseInteger(qs.num.value);
      if (n == null) return;
      const r = MT.Hissan.answer(hs, n);
      if (!r.correct) {
        UI.se('wrong');
        qs.num.clear();
        if (r.fails >= 3) flash(wrap, '答えは ' + r.step.answer + '。写して 進もう');
        else flash(wrap, 'もう一度');
        drawRight();
        return;
      }
      UI.se('tap');
      qs.num.clear();
      if (r.done) {
        qs.hissan = null;
        qs.num.value = String(r.result);
        ctx.render();
        flash(document.querySelector('.board-wrap'), '筆算で ' + r.result + ' が 出た。答える を おそう');
        return;
      }
      ctx.render();
    }

    function onCut(segs) {
      if (q.format === 'line') {
        segs.forEach(function (s) { if (dungeonMain) E.undergroundAddLine(s); else E.guideAddLine(s); });
        ctx.render();
        return;
      }
      if (guided) {
        const snapshot = guide.lines.slice();
        let bad = null;
        for (let i = 0; i < segs.length; i += 1) {
          const r = E.guideAddLine(segs[i]);
          if (!r.ok) { bad = r.message || 'そこでは 切れないよ'; break; }
        }
        if (bad) {
          Guide.clearLines(guide);
          snapshot.forEach(function (l) { E.guideAddLine(l); });
          ctx.render();
          flash(document.querySelector('.board-wrap'), bad);
          return;
        }
        if (guide.mode === 'cutonly' && guide.finished) { submitAnswer(String(q.answer)); return; }
        ctx.render();
        return;
      }
      if (dungeonMain) segs.forEach(function (s) { E.undergroundAddLine(s); });
      else qs.lines = qs.lines.concat(segs);
      ctx.render();
    }

    function clearLines() {
      if (q.format === 'line') { if (dungeonMain) E.undergroundClearLines(); else E.guideClear(); }
      else if (guided) { E.guideClear(); qs.help = null; }
      else if (dungeonMain) E.undergroundClearLines();
      else qs.lines = [];
      ctx.render();
    }

    function submitAnswer(given) {
      const sessIndex = sess ? sess.index : null;
      const snapshotLines = (lines || []).slice();
      const res = dungeonMain ? E.undergroundAnswer(given) : E.answer(given);
      if (!res) return;
      const empty = dungeonMain ? !!res.empty : !!(res.result && res.result.empty);
      if (empty) { flash(wrap, '答えを 入れてね'); return; }
      const correct = dungeonMain ? !!res.correct : !!res.result.correct;
      UI.se(correct ? 'correct' : 'wrong');
      let fbText = '';
      if (correct) {
        fbText = formulaText(q) || (q.explain && q.explain.sentence) || '';
        // 地下の解き方には 座標（（0〜3）など）が入るので 子どもには 見せない。長すぎるときは 答えだけ
        fbText = fbText.replace(/（\d+〜\d+）/g, '');
        if (dungeonMain && fbText.length > 64) fbText = q.format === 'fields' ? '' : '答えは ' + q.answer + (q.unit || '') + '。';
      }
      else if (dungeonMain) fbText = (q.format === 'line' && res.message ? res.message + ' ' : '') + 'どこが ちがう? もう一度。' + (res.help ? '　ヒント: ' + res.help : '');
      else if (res.stumbled) fbText = res.message;
      else if (q.format === 'line' && res.message) fbText = res.message;
      st.hold = { key: qs.key, q: q, correct: correct, given: given, res: res, dungeonMain: dungeonMain, guide: guide, lines: snapshotLines, ugNo: ugNo, fbText: fbText, sessIndex: sessIndex };
      ctx.render();
    }

    function next() {
      const hd = st.hold;
      st.hold = null;
      if (hd && hd.dungeonMain && !hd.correct) {
        qs.num.clear(); qs.numState = ''; qs.choice = null; qs.fields = {};
      } else {
        st.qs = null;
      }
      ctx.render();
    }

    // 管理者の ためしプレイ: こたえを 画面の 上に 出す
    if (st.admin && st.adminShowAnswer && !hold && UI.adminAnswerText) scr.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'admin-answer', plain: '答え： ' + UI.adminAnswerText(q, step, finalChoiceNow) }));

    ctx.setKey(function (k) {
      if (hold) { if (k === 'enter') next(); return; }
      if (finalChoiceNow || q.format === 'choice') {
        const choices = finalChoiceNow ? q.finalChoice.choices : q.choices;
        if (/^[1-9]$/.test(k) && choices[Number(k) - 1]) { qs.choice = choices[Number(k) - 1].id; drawRight(); return; }
        if (k === 'enter' && qs.choice != null) submitAnswer(qs.choice);
        return;
      }
      if (q.format === 'order') { if (k === 'enter') submitAnswer(qs.order.slice()); return; }
      if (q.format === 'line') { if (k === 'enter' && lines.length) submitAnswer(null); return; }
      if (guided && !step) return;
      typeKey(k);
    });

    app.appendChild(scr);
  };
})(typeof globalThis !== 'undefined' ? globalThis : this);


/* 面積の塔: まちがい直し
 * 1. 解説（天使ちゃん）: 図は 正しい見方を書き込んだ静止画。あなうめで 手順を 自分でたどる
 * 2. やり直しモード: 赤いわく。元の問題に もう一度 自分で答える。せいかいするまで 先へ進めない
 */
(function (root) {
  'use strict';
  const MT = root.MT;
  const UI = MT.UI;
  const h = UI.h;
  const Guide = MT.Guide;
  UI.screens = UI.screens || {};

  // 行の 色と、図の その長方形の わくの 色を そろえる（外と内が どこか わからない。本人の指摘 2026-09-16）
  const ROW_COLORS = {
    '左': '#6cbd6f', '右': '#ef9448', 'まん中': '#a48ad0',
    '外': '#3b7dd8', '内': '#ef9448', 'ひく': '#a48ad0', '外がわ': '#3b7dd8', '内がわ': '#ef9448',
    'かこむ大きい長方形': '#3b7dd8', '欠けたところ': '#ef9448', '道をよせる': '#6cbd6f', '横': '#6cbd6f'
  };
  const FIG_TWO = ['#6cbd6f', '#ef9448'];

  function itemKeyOf(v) { return [v.item.index, v.item.templateId, v.item.seed].join(':'); }

  function newRs(key, q) {
    return {
      key: key, stepKey: null, value: '', ng: false,
      choice: null, order: q.order ? q.order.items.map(function (i) { return i.id; }) : null,
      pairs: [], pairA: '', pairB: '', pairFocus: 'a',
      fields: {}, fieldFocus: q.fields && q.fields.length ? q.fields[0].key : null,
      tiles: new Set(), lines: [], fb: null, hissan: null
    };
  }

  // やり直しモードの 選択肢は、はじめの 問題と ならびを かえる（まちがえるたびに また かえる）
  function shuffleChoices(list, rs) {
    const arr = list.slice();
    let s = ((rs && rs.shuffle) || 0) * 7919 + 104729;
    for (let i = arr.length - 1; i > 0; i -= 1) {
      s = (s * 9301 + 49297) % 233280;
      const j = Math.floor(s / 233280 * (i + 1));
      const t = arr[i]; arr[i] = arr[j]; arr[j] = t;
    }
    return arr;
  }

  function yaPill(label, value, strike, small) {
    return h('div', { class: 'ya-pill' + (strike ? ' strike' : '') + (small ? ' small' : '') }, [h('span', { class: 'l', text: label }), h('span', { class: 'v', text: value || '（なし）' })]);
  }

  // ホームへ（まちがい直しは のこる。ホームの「つづける」で ここに もどる）
  function homeBtn(ctx) {
    return h('button', { class: 'back-btn', type: 'button', 'aria-label': 'ホームへ', onclick: function () {
      const held = ctx.state.retryHold;
      ctx.state.retryHold = null;
      // さいごの 問題が とおった直後は、クリア（または 差し戻し）の画面を 先に 見せる。そこに ホームへ がある
      if (held && held.finished) { ctx.render(); return; }
      ctx.engine.goMap();
      ctx.render();
    } }, ['◀']);
  }

  function colorize(text, pairs) {
    if (!pairs.length) return [UI.tx(text)];
    const esc = function (s) { return s.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, '\\$&'); };
    const re = new RegExp('(' + pairs.map(function (p) { return esc(p.word); }).join('|') + ')', 'g');
    return String(text).split(re).filter(function (x) { return x !== ''; }).map(function (part) {
      const pp = pairs.filter(function (x) { return x.word === part; })[0];
      return pp ? h('span', { class: 'say-part', style: 'color:' + pp.color, text: part }) : UI.tx(part);
    });
  }

  function partName(ask) {
    const m = /^(.*?)の (たて|よこ|面積)は\?$/.exec(ask || '');
    let n = m ? m[1] : '';
    n = n.replace(/の長方形$/, '');
    if (n === 'かこむ大きい長方形') n = '大きい 四角';
    return n;
  }

  UI.screens.retry = function (ctx) {
    const E = ctx.engine, st = ctx.state;
    if (st.retryHold) { renderRedo(ctx, st.retryHold.v, st.retryHold, null, st.retryHold.finalPending); return; }
    const v = E.retryView();
    if (!v) { E.goMap(); ctx.render(); return; }
    const key = itemKeyOf(v);
    if (!st.rs || st.rs.key !== key) st.rs = newRs(key, v.question);
    const rs = st.rs;
    if (rs.stepKey !== v.stepIndex) { rs.stepKey = v.stepIndex; rs.value = ''; rs.ng = false; rs.fb = null; }
    const g = v.step && v.step.kind === 'cut' ? E.retryGuide() : null;
    const finalPending = !!(g && g.guide.finished && v.question.finalChoice);
    const redoStage = v.isReanswer || finalPending;
    if (redoStage && (st.explainShown[key] || st.previewRedo)) { renderRedo(ctx, v, null, g, finalPending); return; }
    renderExplain(ctx, v, g, redoStage, key);
  };

  // ================= 解説 =================
  function renderExplain(ctx, v, g, doneMode, key) {
    const E = ctx.engine, st = ctx.state, rs = st.rs, q = v.question;
    const sess = E.session();
    UI.bg('explain', {}, { tint: 'rgba(31,39,64,0.35)' });
    const app = document.getElementById('app');
    const scr = h('div', { class: 'screen' });
    const layout = h('div', { class: 'q-layout' });
    scr.appendChild(layout);
    layout.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'q-head' }, [
      h('div', { class: 'q-head-left' }, [homeBtn(ctx), h('div', { class: 'floor-label outline-text', plain: UI.floorLabelOf(sess) }), h('div', { class: 'head-tag', text: 'まちがい直し' })]),
      h('div', { class: 'q-head-right' }, [yaPill('君の答え', v.yourAnswer)])
    ]));
    const body = h('div', { class: 'q-body explain-body' });
    layout.appendChild(body);
    const left = h('div', { class: 'q-left' });
    body.appendChild(left);

    // 切ったあとの 解説（そのとおり）でも、色をぬった図と うめた行を 見せたままにする
    const guide = g ? g.guide : (doneMode && rs.guideSnap ? rs.guideSnap : null);
    const parts = guide && guide.analysis && (guide.analysis.mode === 'cut' || guide.analysis.mode === 'enclose') ? Guide.partsForDisplay(guide) : [];
    // 色の対応（図・行のしるし・ふきだしの言葉）
    const chipOf = {};
    let ci = 0;
    parts.forEach(function (pp, i) {
      if (pp.role === 'box') chipOf[i] = '#3b7dd8';
      else if (pp.role === 'cutout') chipOf[i] = '#b9b5a4';
      else { chipOf[i] = MT.Board.GUIDE_FILLS[ci % 4]; ci += 1; }
    });
    const colorPairs = [];
    if (guide && guide.steps) {
      guide.steps.forEach(function (s) {
        if (s.part == null) return;
        const nm = partName(s.ask);
        if (nm && chipOf[s.part] && !colorPairs.some(function (x) { return x.word === nm + 'の長方形'; })) colorPairs.push({ word: nm + 'の長方形', color: chipOf[s.part] === '#6cbd6f' ? '#3d6a55' : chipOf[s.part] === '#ef9448' ? '#c2661f' : chipOf[s.part] });
      });
    }
    if (q.figures && q.figures.length === 2) { colorPairs.push({ word: '左は', color: '#3d6a55' }); colorPairs.push({ word: '右は', color: '#c2661f' }); }

    // 自分で あなうめや 切る問題を やりおえたか（選ぶだけの 問題は、まちがえた 直後に この 画面が 出る）
    const didWork = !!g || !!rs.guideSnap || v.steps.slice(0, v.stepIndex).some(function (s) { return !MT.Retry.isReanswerStep(s); });
    // ふきだし
    const hsEngine = v.hissan;
    const say = [];
    if (guide && guide.mode === 'three' && guide.way && !guide.finished) say.push(h('span', { class: 'step-ask', style: 'margin-top:0;margin-bottom:0.3vh', text: '「' + Guide.wayName(guide.way) + '」で 分けたよ。' }));
    if (hsEngine || rs.hissan) {
      const hp = hsEngine || rs.hissan;
      if (MT.Hissan.isDone(hp)) say.push(h('span', { class: 'say-ok', text: '筆算が できた。答えは ' + hp.answer + '。' }));
      else say.push(UI.tx('式は 合っているよ。計算を 筆算で たしかめよう。'));
    } else if (v.revealed) {
      // どこに 写すかを はっきり（「答えは 2cm」だけでは、面積の 答えなのか どの □ なのか 分からなかった。2026-10-07）
      const rv = E.retryReveal();
      const where = doneMode ? '答えは ' : '光っている □ に 入るのは ';
      say.push(h('span', { class: 'say-reveal', text: where + (rv ? rv.text : '') + '。写して 進もう。次の階で もう一度 出るよ。' }));
    } else if (v.help) {
      say.push(h('span', { class: 'say-help', text: v.help.text }));
    } else if (doneMode) {
      // 「そのとおり」は あなうめや 切る問題を 自分で やりおえた ときだけ。選ぶだけの 問題は まちがえた 直後なので 言わない
      if (didWork) {
        say.push(h('span', { class: 'say-ok', text: 'そのとおり。' }));
        say.push(h('br'));
      }
      colorize(v.sentence, colorPairs).forEach(function (n) { say.push(n); });
    } else {
      colorize(v.sentence, colorPairs).forEach(function (n) { say.push(n); });
    }
    const sayText = say.map(function (n) { return n.textContent || ''; }).join('');
    left.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'speaker-row' }, [UI.guardBox('angel', 'light'), h('div', { class: 'bubble' + UI.bubbleSizeClass(sayText) }, say)]));
    const pcE = UI.peerCard(q);
    if (pcE) left.appendChild(pcE);

    // 板: 正しい見方を書き込んだ状態
    const wrap = h('div', { class: 'board-wrap' });
    left.appendChild(wrap);
    let model = null;
    if (guide) {
      model = { figures: [q.figure], showHidden: true, partColors: MT.Board.GUIDE_FILLS, lines: guide.lines, innerGrid: false };
      if (parts.length) model.parts = parts;
      const cs = Guide.currentStep(guide);
      if (cs && cs.part != null) model.activePart = cs.part;
      if (cs && cs.dim) model.litDims = [cs.dim];
    } else {
      const fm = UI.figuresModel(q);
      if (fm) {
        model = Object.assign({ showHidden: true }, fm);
        if (fm.figures && fm.figures.length === 2) { model.figColors = FIG_TWO; model.nameColors = ['#3d6a55', '#c2661f']; }
        // タイルを おく問題の 解説は、ぜんぶ おいた ようすを 見せる
        if (q.figure && q.figure.interaction === 'tiles' && model.figures) model.tiles = MT.Figure.cellSet(q.figure.fig);
      }
    }
    if (model && v.help && v.help.segments && v.help.segments.length) model.helpSegs = v.help.segments;
    // いま うめる □ が 図の どこかを 赤い 太線で 見せる（文字だけでは わかりにくい。本人の指摘 2026-09-16）
    if (model && !guide) {
      const ob = openBlank();
      const src = ob && (ob.dim || ob.area) ? ob : (v.step && (v.step.dim || v.step.area) ? v.step : null);
      if (src && src.dim) {
        const d = src.dim;
        if (d.whole && q.figure && q.figure.fig) {
          const bb = MT.Figure.bounds(q.figure.fig);
          model.litDims = [d.axis === 'y' ? { axis: 'y', a: bb.y1, b: bb.y2 } : { axis: 'x', a: bb.x1, b: bb.x2 }];
        } else if (d.a != null) model.litDims = [d];
      }
      if (src && src.area) model.litArea = true;
      // 「外」「内」など、いまの 行が 図の どの長方形かを 同じ色の わくで 見せる
      if (v.step && v.step.rect) {
        model.litRect = v.step.rect;
        model.litRectColor = ROW_COLORS[v.step.label] || null;
      }
    }
    UI.mountBoard(wrap, model, q.prompt);
    const hp = hsEngine || rs.hissan;
    if (hp) wrap.appendChild(UI.hissanOverlay(hp));

    // 右: あなうめ
    let right = buildRight();
    body.appendChild(right);
    function redrawRight() { const n = buildRight(); right.replaceWith(n); right = n; }

    function openBlank() {
      if (!v.step || v.step.kind || !Array.isArray(v.step.parts)) return null;
      const bl = v.step.parts.filter(function (p) { return p && typeof p === 'object' && p.blank; });
      for (let i = 0; i < bl.length; i += 1) if (!v.item.blankDone[v.step.key + '.' + bl[i].blank]) return bl[i];
      return null;
    }

    function target() {
      if (doneMode) return null;
      if (hsEngine && !MT.Hissan.isDone(hsEngine)) { const hs = MT.Hissan.current(hsEngine); return hs ? { kind: 'hissan', answer: hs.answer } : null; }
      if (hsEngine) return null;
      if (rs.hissan) { const hs = MT.Hissan.current(rs.hissan); return hs ? { kind: 'ghissan', answer: hs.answer } : null; }
      if (guide) { const cs = Guide.currentStep(guide); return cs ? { kind: 'guide', answer: cs.answer } : null; }
      const b = openBlank();
      return b ? { kind: 'blank', blank: b.blank, answer: b.answer } : null;
    }

    function blankBtn(text, state) {
      return h('button', { class: 'blank-btn ' + state, type: 'button' }, [text]);
    }

    function fillRows() {
      const rows = [];
      if (guide) {
        const steps = guide.steps || [];
        if (!steps.length) return rows;
        const byPart = {}, order = [];
        steps.forEach(function (s, idx) { if (s.part == null) return; if (!byPart[s.part]) { byPart[s.part] = []; order.push(s.part); } byPart[s.part].push({ s: s, idx: idx }); });
        const cur = guide.stepIndex;
        const bOf = function (s, idx) {
          if (idx < cur) return blankBtn(String(s.answer), 'ok');
          if (idx === cur && !guide.finished) {
            if (rs.hissan) return blankBtn('筆算', 'on');
            return blankBtn(rs.value || '□', 'on' + (rs.ng ? ' ng' : ''));
          }
          return blankBtn('□', 'wait');
        };
        order.forEach(function (pi) {
          const list = byPart[pi];
          const kids = [h('span', { class: 'chip', style: 'background:' + (chipOf[pi] || '#5d8fd8') }), h('span', { class: 'row-label', text: partName(list[0].s.ask) })];
          list.forEach(function (it) {
            // □ の 前に たて・横 を 書く（どっちを 先に 入れるか 分からなかった。2026-10-07）
            if (/-h$/.test(it.s.key)) { kids.push(h('span', { class: 'fill-part', text: 'たて' })); kids.push(bOf(it.s, it.idx)); }
            else if (/-w$/.test(it.s.key)) { kids.push(h('span', { class: 'fill-part', plain: '×' })); kids.push(h('span', { class: 'fill-part', text: '横' })); kids.push(bOf(it.s, it.idx)); }
            else { if (list.length > 1) kids.push(h('span', { class: 'fill-part', plain: '＝' })); kids.push(bOf(it.s, it.idx)); kids.push(h('span', { class: 'blank-unit', plain: it.s.unit || '' })); }
          });
          const allDone = list.every(function (it) { return it.idx < cur; });
          if (allDone) kids.push(h('span', { class: 'mark', plain: '✓' }));
          rows.push(h('div', { class: 'fill-row ' + (allDone ? 'done' : list.some(function (it) { return it.idx === cur; }) ? 'active' : 'todo') }, kids));
        });
        const last = steps[steps.length - 1];
        if (last && last.part == null) {
          const idx = steps.length - 1;
          const areas = steps.filter(function (s) { return /-area$/.test(s.key); });
          const kids = [h('span', { class: 'chip' }), h('span', { class: 'row-label', text: last.key === 'sub' ? 'ひく' : '全部' })];
          areas.forEach(function (s, j) {
            if (j) kids.push(h('span', { class: 'fill-part', plain: last.key === 'sub' ? '−' : '＋' }));
            kids.push(h('span', { class: 'fill-part', plain: steps.indexOf(s) < cur ? String(s.answer) : '□' }));
          });
          kids.push(h('span', { class: 'fill-part', plain: '＝' }));
          kids.push(bOf(last, idx));
          kids.push(h('span', { class: 'blank-unit', plain: last.unit || '' }));
          if (idx < cur || guide.finished) kids.push(h('span', { class: 'mark', plain: '✓' }));
          rows.push(h('div', { class: 'fill-row ' + (idx < cur || guide.finished ? 'done' : idx === cur ? 'active' : 'todo') }, kids));
        }
        return rows;
      }
      const ob = openBlank();
      v.steps.forEach(function (s, i) {
        if (s.kind || !Array.isArray(s.parts)) return;
        const blanks = s.parts.filter(function (p) { return p && typeof p === 'object' && p.blank; });
        const allDone = blanks.length > 0 && blanks.every(function (b) { return v.item.blankDone[s.key + '.' + b.blank]; });
        const state = allDone || i < v.stepIndex ? 'done' : i === v.stepIndex ? 'active' : 'todo';
        const kids = [h('span', { class: 'chip', style: 'background:' + (ROW_COLORS[s.label] || '#5d8fd8') }), h('span', { class: 'row-label', text: s.label || '' })];
        s.parts.forEach(function (p) {
          if (typeof p === 'string') { kids.push(h('span', { class: 'fill-part', text: p })); return; }
          if (!p || !p.blank) return;
          const done = state === 'done' || v.item.blankDone[s.key + '.' + p.blank];
          if (done) kids.push(blankBtn(String(p.answer), 'ok'));
          else if (state === 'active' && ob && ob.blank === p.blank) {
            if (hsEngine) kids.push(blankBtn(MT.Hissan.isDone(hsEngine) ? String(hsEngine.answer) : '筆算', MT.Hissan.isDone(hsEngine) ? 'ok' : 'on'));
            else kids.push(blankBtn(rs.value || '□', 'on' + (rs.ng ? ' ng' : '')));
          } else kids.push(blankBtn('□', 'wait'));
          if (p.unit) kids.push(h('span', { class: 'blank-unit ' + UI.unitKind(p.unit), plain: p.unit }));
        });
        if (state === 'done') kids.push(h('span', { class: 'mark', plain: '✓' }));
        rows.push(h('div', { class: 'fill-row ' + state }, kids));
      });
      return rows;
    }

    function buildRight() {
      const col = h('div', { class: 'q-right' });
      const rows = fillRows();
      if (rows.length) col.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'fill-rows' }, rows));
      else col.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'outcome-note', text: q.prompt }));
      const t = target();
      if (t) {
        if (t.kind === 'hissan' || t.kind === 'ghissan') {
          const hs = MT.Hissan.current(t.kind === 'hissan' ? hsEngine : rs.hissan);
          col.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'hs-ask', text: (hs.label ? hs.label + '　' : '') + hs.expr + ' ＝ ' + (rs.value || '?') }));
        }
        col.appendChild(UI.keypad(onKey));
      } else {
        col.appendChild(h('div', { style: 'flex:1' }));
      }
      let nextBtn;
      if (hsEngine && MT.Hissan.isDone(hsEngine)) nextBtn = h('button', { class: 'answer-btn next', type: 'button', onclick: function () { E.retryHissanConfirm(); ctx.render(); } }, ['次へ　▶']);
      else if (doneMode) nextBtn = h('button', { class: 'answer-btn next', type: 'button', onclick: goRedo }, [didWork ? '次へ　▶' : 'もう一度 答える　▶']);
      // けた数が 足りなくても 自分で 出せるように（タッチだけでも すすめる）
      else if (t && rs.value) nextBtn = h('button', { class: 'answer-btn', type: 'button', onclick: function () { const tt = target(); if (tt && rs.value) submit(tt); } }, ['答える']);
      else nextBtn = h('button', { class: 'answer-btn wait', type: 'button' }, [UI.tx('□ を うめると 進める')]);
      col.appendChild(nextBtn);
      return col;
    }

    function goRedo() { st.explainShown[key] = true; ctx.render(); }

    function onKey(k) {
      const t = target();
      if (!t) {
        if (k === 'enter') {
          if (hsEngine && MT.Hissan.isDone(hsEngine)) { E.retryHissanConfirm(); ctx.render(); }
          else if (doneMode) goRedo();
        }
        return;
      }
      if (k === 'back') { rs.value = rs.value.slice(0, -1); rs.ng = false; redrawRight(); return; }
      if (k === 'clear') { rs.value = ''; rs.ng = false; redrawRight(); return; }
      if (k === 'enter') { if (rs.value) submit(t); return; }
      if (rs.ng) { rs.value = ''; rs.ng = false; }
      if (rs.value.length >= 6) return;
      rs.value = (rs.value + k).replace(/^0+(?=\d)/, '');
      if (rs.value.length >= String(t.answer).length) submit(t);
      else redrawRight();
    }

    function submit(t) {
      if (t.kind === 'ghissan') {
        const r = MT.Hissan.answer(rs.hissan, MT.Util.parseInteger(rs.value));
        if (!r.correct) { UI.se('wrong'); rs.ng = true; if (r.fails >= 3) UI.flash(wrap, '答えは ' + r.step.answer + '。写して 進もう'); redrawRight(); return; }
        UI.se('tap');
        if (r.done) { const result = r.result; rs.hissan = null; rs.value = String(result); rs.ng = false; submit({ kind: 'guide', answer: result }); return; }
        rs.value = '';
        ctx.render();
        return;
      }
      let res;
      if (t.kind === 'hissan') res = E.retryHissan(rs.value);
      else if (t.kind === 'guide') {
        const before = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(guide));
        res = E.retryGuideAnswer(rs.value);
        if (res && res.correct && (res.guideFinished || res.needFinalChoice)) rs.guideSnap = Object.assign(before, { stepIndex: before.steps.length, finished: true });
      }
      else res = E.retryBlank(t.blank, rs.value);
      if (!res || res.empty) return;
      if (!res.correct) {
        UI.se('wrong');
        rs.ng = true;
        if (t.kind === 'guide' && res.calc && !rs.hissan) { rs.hissan = MT.Hissan.plan(res.calc[0], res.calc[1], res.calc[2]); rs.value = ''; rs.ng = false; }
        ctx.render();
        return;
      }
      UI.se(res.itemDone ? 'correct' : 'tap');
      rs.value = ''; rs.ng = false;
      ctx.render();
    }

    if (st.admin && st.adminShowAnswer) { const rv = E.retryReveal(); if (rv) scr.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'admin-answer', plain: '答え： ' + rv.text })); }
    ctx.setKey(onKey);
    app.appendChild(scr);
  }

  // ================= やり直しモード =================
  function renderRedo(ctx, v, held, g, finalPending) {
    const E = ctx.engine, st = ctx.state, q = v.question;
    const sess = E.session();
    const rs = held ? held.rs : st.rs;
    const floorLabel = held ? held.floorLabel : UI.floorLabelOf(sess);
    const guardId = held ? held.guardId : UI.guardOfSession(sess);
    UI.bg('redo', {}, { tint: 'rgba(138,42,30,0.42)' });
    const app = document.getElementById('app');
    const scr = h('div', { class: 'screen' }, [h('div', { class: 'redo-frame' }), h('div', { class: 'redo-stripe top' }), h('div', { class: 'redo-stripe bottom' })]);
    const layout = h('div', { class: 'q-layout redo-layout' });
    scr.appendChild(layout);
    const total = v.redoTotal, idx = v.redoIndex - 1;
    const marks = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < total; i += 1) {
      const done = i < idx || (held && i === idx);
      marks.push(h('div', { class: 'm' + (done ? ' done' : i === idx ? ' now' : '') }, [done ? '✓' : '']));
    }
    layout.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'q-head' }, [
      h('div', { class: 'q-head-left' }, [
        homeBtn(ctx),
        h('div', { class: 'floor-label outline-text', plain: floorLabel }),
        h('div', { class: 'redo-tag' }, [h('span', { class: 'stripe-chip' }), UI.tx('やり直しモード')]),
        h('div', { class: 'redo-note', text: 'せいかいするまで 先へ 進めない' })
      ]),
      h('div', { class: 'q-head-right' }, [h('div', { class: 'redo-marks' }, marks), h('div', { class: 'q-count', plain: (idx + 1) + '／' + total })])
    ]));
    const body = h('div', { class: 'q-body' });
    layout.appendChild(body);
    const left = h('div', { class: 'q-left' });
    body.appendChild(left);

    const kind = finalPending ? 'final' : v.step.kind;
    const say = [UI.tx('もう一度。'), UI.tx(q.prompt)];
    if (finalPending) say.push(h('span', { class: 'step-ask', text: q.finalChoice.prompt }));
    // 問題文が もう「どこが ちがう?」と 聞いて いるときは、同じ 問いを 2回 出さない
    else if (kind === 'choice' && v.step.label && v.step.label.indexOf('もう一度') !== 0 && !(/どこが/.test(v.step.label) && /どこが/.test(q.prompt))) say.push(h('span', { class: 'step-ask', text: v.step.label }));
    // 答えを見せている問題（1つの問題で 3回まちがえた）は、やり直しモードでも はじめから 答えを出しておく
    if (v.revealed && !rs.fb) { const rv = E.retryReveal(); if (rv) say.push(h('span', { class: 'step-ask say-reveal', text: '答えは ' + rv.text + '。写して 進もう。' })); }
    if (held) say.splice(0, say.length, h('span', { class: 'say-ok', text: 'よし。通って よい。' }));
    const sayText = say.map(function (n) { return n.textContent || ''; }).join('');
    left.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'speaker-row' }, [UI.guardBox(guardId), h('div', { class: 'bubble' + UI.bubbleSizeClass(sayText) }, say)]));
    const pcR = UI.peerCard(q);
    if (pcR) left.appendChild(pcR);
    left.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'ya-row' }, [yaPill('さっきの答え', v.yourAnswer, true, true)]));

    const wrap = h('div', { class: 'board-wrap redo' + (held ? ' ok' : (rs.fb ? ' ng' : '')) });
    left.appendChild(wrap);
    const fm = UI.figuresModel(q);
    let model = null;
    let board = null;
    if (fm) {
      model = Object.assign({ showHidden: true }, fm);
      if (!model.tilingDemo && kind === 'reanswer') {
        if (q.format === 'grid' && q.figure && q.figure.interaction === 'tiles') {
          model.tiles = rs.tiles;
          const pre = MT.Board.placedCells(q.figure);
          if (!held) model.onTile = function (k, add) { if (pre.has(k)) return; if (add) rs.tiles.add(k); else rs.tiles.delete(k); board.set(model); };
        }
        if (q.format === 'cut' || (q.figure && q.figure.interaction === 'cut')) {
          model.lines = rs.lines;
          if (!held) model.onCut = function (segs) { rs.lines = rs.lines.concat(segs); model.lines = rs.lines; board.set(model); };
        }
      }
    }
    board = UI.mountBoard(wrap, model, q.prompt);
    if (held) {
      UI.stamp(true).forEach(function (n) { wrap.appendChild(n); });
      wrap.appendChild(UI.band(true, '通った！', held.finished ? 'これで まちがい直しは 終わり。' : '次の 問題へ。'));
    } else if (rs.fb) {
      UI.stamp(false).forEach(function (n) { wrap.appendChild(n); });
      wrap.appendChild(UI.band(false, 'まだ ちがう', rs.fb.text, { small: true }));
    }

    let right = buildRight();
    body.appendChild(right);
    function redrawRight() { const n = buildRight(); right.replaceWith(n); right = n; }

    function buildRight() {
      const col = h('div', { class: 'q-right' });
      const locked = !!held || !!rs.fb;
      if (kind === 'final' || kind === 'choice') {
        const choices = shuffleChoices(kind === 'final' ? q.finalChoice.choices : q.choices, rs);
        col.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'col-label', text: '一つ 選ぼう' }));
        col.appendChild(UI.choiceList(choices, { selected: rs.choice, held: locked, given: held ? held.given : rs.lastGiven, correct: !!held, onPick: function (id) { rs.choice = id; redrawRight(); } }));
      } else if (kind === 'order') {
        col.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'col-label', text: '1が 一番 小さい' }));
        col.appendChild(UI.orderList(q.order.items, rs.order, { held: locked, onSwap: function (a, b) { const t = rs.order[a]; rs.order[a] = rs.order[b]; rs.order[b] = t; redrawRight(); } }));
      } else if (kind === 'pairs') {
        col.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'fill-rows' }, [h('div', { class: 'fill-row active' }, [
          h('span', { class: 'row-label', text: 'たて' }),
          h('button', { class: 'blank-btn' + (rs.pairFocus === 'a' ? ' on' : ''), type: 'button', onclick: function () { rs.pairFocus = 'a'; redrawRight(); } }, [rs.pairA || '□']),
          h('span', { class: 'fill-part', plain: '×' }),
          h('span', { class: 'row-label', text: '横' }),
          h('button', { class: 'blank-btn' + (rs.pairFocus === 'b' ? ' on' : ''), type: 'button', onclick: function () { rs.pairFocus = 'b'; redrawRight(); } }, [rs.pairB || '□']),
          h('button', { class: 'px-btn pair-add', type: 'button', onclick: addPair }, [UI.tx('入れる')])
        ])]));
        col.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'pairs-found' }, rs.pairs.map(function (pr) { return h('span', { class: 'pair-chip', plain: pr[0] + '×' + pr[1] }); })));
        col.appendChild(UI.keypad(onKey));
      } else if (q.format === 'fields') {
        col.appendChild(UI.fieldRows(q.fields, rs.fields, rs.fieldFocus, { held: locked, onFocus: function (k) { rs.fieldFocus = k; redrawRight(); } }));
        col.appendChild(UI.keypad(onKey));
      } else {
        if (UI.unitKind(q.unit)) col.appendChild(UI.askTag(q.unit));
        col.appendChild(UI.numDisplay(rs.value, q.unit || '', { state: held ? 'ok' : (rs.fb ? 'ng' : '') }));
        col.appendChild(UI.keypad(onKey));
      }
      let btn;
      if (held) btn = h('button', { class: 'answer-btn next', type: 'button', onclick: nextItem }, ['次へ　▶']);
      else if (rs.fb) btn = h('button', { class: 'answer-btn again', type: 'button', onclick: again }, ['もう一度']);
      else btn = h('button', { class: 'answer-btn' + (ready() ? '' : ' disabled'), type: 'button', onclick: function () { if (ready()) submit(); } }, ['答える']);
      col.appendChild(btn);
      return col;
    }

    function ready() {
      if (kind === 'final' || kind === 'choice') return rs.choice != null;
      if (kind === 'order') return true;
      if (kind === 'pairs') return rs.pairs.length > 0;
      if (q.format === 'fields') return q.fields.some(function (f) { return (rs.fields[f.key] || '') !== ''; });
      return rs.value !== '';
    }

    function addPair() {
      const x = MT.Util.parseInteger(rs.pairA), y = MT.Util.parseInteger(rs.pairB);
      if (x == null || y == null || x <= 0 || y <= 0) return;
      if (x * y !== q.pairs.area) { UI.se('wrong'); UI.flash(wrap, x + '×' + y + '＝' + (x * y) + '。' + q.pairs.area + ' に ならない'); rs.pairA = ''; rs.pairB = ''; rs.pairFocus = 'a'; redrawRight(); return; }
      const lo = Math.min(x, y), hi = Math.max(x, y);
      if (!rs.pairs.some(function (pr) { return pr[0] === lo && pr[1] === hi; })) rs.pairs.push([lo, hi]);
      rs.pairA = ''; rs.pairB = ''; rs.pairFocus = 'a';
      redrawRight();
    }

    function submit() {
      let res, given;
      if (kind === 'final') { given = rs.choice; res = E.retryGuideFinalChoice(given); }
      else if (kind === 'choice') { given = rs.choice; res = E.retryChoice(given); }
      else if (kind === 'order') { given = rs.order.slice(); res = E.retryOrder(given); }
      else if (kind === 'pairs') { given = rs.pairs.slice(); res = E.retryPairs(given); }
      else if (q.format === 'fields') { given = {}; q.fields.forEach(function (f) { given[f.key] = rs.fields[f.key] || ''; }); res = E.retryReanswer(given); }
      else { given = rs.value; res = E.retryReanswer(given); }
      if (!res || res.empty) return;
      if (!res.correct) {
        UI.se('wrong');
        let text;
        if (res.reveal) { const rv = E.retryReveal(); text = rv ? '答えは ' + rv.text + '。写して 進もう。次の階で もう一度 出るよ。' : ''; }
        else if (res.help) text = res.help.text;
        else text = MT.Bank.explanationFor(q, { misconception: res.misconception || v.item.misconception });
        rs.fb = { text: text };
        rs.lastGiven = given;
        ctx.render();
        return;
      }
      UI.se('correct');
      st.retryHold = { v: v, rs: rs, given: given, finished: E.phase() === 'outcome', floorLabel: floorLabel, guardId: guardId, finalPending: finalPending };
      st.rs = null;
      ctx.render();
    }

    function again() {
      rs.fb = null; rs.value = ''; rs.choice = null; rs.lastGiven = null; rs.pairA = ''; rs.pairB = '';
      rs.shuffle = (rs.shuffle || 0) + 1;   // もう一度 えらぶときは ならびを かえる（場所で 覚えないように）
      if (q.format === 'fields') { rs.fields = {}; rs.fieldFocus = q.fields[0].key; }
      ctx.render();
    }

    function nextItem() { st.retryHold = null; ctx.render(); }

    if (st.admin && st.adminShowAnswer && !held) { const rv = E.retryReveal(); if (rv) scr.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'admin-answer', plain: '答え： ' + rv.text })); }

    function onKey(k) {
      if (held) { if (k === 'enter') nextItem(); return; }
      if (rs.fb) { if (k === 'enter') again(); return; }
      if (kind === 'final' || kind === 'choice') {
        const choices = shuffleChoices(kind === 'final' ? q.finalChoice.choices : q.choices, rs);
        if (/^[1-9]$/.test(k) && choices[Number(k) - 1]) { rs.choice = choices[Number(k) - 1].id; redrawRight(); return; }
        if (k === 'enter' && ready()) submit();
        return;
      }
      if (kind === 'order') { if (k === 'enter') submit(); return; }
      if (kind === 'pairs') {
        if (k === 'enter') { if (rs.pairA && !rs.pairB) { rs.pairFocus = 'b'; redrawRight(); } else if (rs.pairA && rs.pairB) addPair(); return; }
        const fk = rs.pairFocus === 'a' ? 'pairA' : 'pairB';
        let val = rs[fk];
        if (k === 'back') val = val.slice(0, -1); else if (k === 'clear') val = ''; else if (val.length < 4) val = (val + k).replace(/^0+(?=\d)/, '');
        rs[fk] = val;
        redrawRight();
        return;
      }
      if (q.format === 'fields') {
        const f = rs.fieldFocus;
        if (k === 'enter') {
          const empty = q.fields.filter(function (x) { return (rs.fields[x.key] || '') === ''; })[0];
          if (empty && (rs.fields[f] || '') !== '') { rs.fieldFocus = empty.key; redrawRight(); return; }
          if (ready()) submit();
          return;
        }
        let val = rs.fields[f] || '';
        if (k === 'back') val = val.slice(0, -1); else if (k === 'clear') val = ''; else if (val.length < 7) val = (val + k).replace(/^0+(?=\d)/, '');
        rs.fields[f] = val;
        redrawRight();
        return;
      }
      if (k === 'enter') { if (ready()) submit(); return; }
      if (k === 'back') rs.value = rs.value.slice(0, -1);
      else if (k === 'clear') rs.value = '';
      else if (rs.value.length < 6) rs.value = (rs.value + k).replace(/^0+(?=\d)/, '');
      redrawRight();
    }

    ctx.setKey(onKey);
    app.appendChild(scr);
  }
})(typeof globalThis !== 'undefined' ? globalThis : this);


/* 面積の塔: 10問の結果と、まちがい直しのあと（クリア・差し戻し・再戦の結果） */
(function (root) {
  'use strict';
  const MT = root.MT;
  const UI = MT.UI;
  const h = UI.h;
  UI.screens = UI.screens || {};

  function quoteOf(line) {
    const m = /^[^「]*「(.*)」$/.exec(line || '');
    return m ? m[1] : (line || '');
  }

  UI.screens.result = function (ctx) {
    const E = ctx.engine, st = ctx.state;
    const r = E.result();
    const sess = E.session();
    if (!r || !sess) { E.goMap(); ctx.render(); return; }
    const rematch = sess.rematch || null;
    const dungeonRematch = sess.floorNo >= 100;
    UI.bg('result', {}, { tint: 'rgba(31,39,64,0.3)' });
    const app = document.getElementById('app');
    const scr = h('div', { class: 'screen' });
    const layout = h('div', { class: 'q-layout' });
    scr.appendChild(layout);
    layout.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'q-head' }, [h('div', { class: 'q-head-left' }, [
      h('div', { class: 'floor-label outline-text', plain: UI.floorLabelOf(sess) }),
      h('div', { class: 'head-tag', text: '結果' }),
      rematch ? h('div', { class: 'head-tag', text: 'ずかんで 戦う' }) : null
    ])]));
    const body = h('div', { class: 'result-body' });
    layout.appendChild(body);

    // 左: 点数・関所・やること
    const left = h('div', { class: 'q-left' });
    body.appendChild(left);
    left.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'score-card' }, [
      h('div', { class: 'lab', text: 'せいかい' }),
      h('div', { style: 'display:flex;align-items:baseline;gap:6px' }, [h('span', { class: 'score-num', plain: String(r.correct) }), h('span', { class: 'score-den', plain: '／' + r.total })]),
      h('div', { class: 'mark-row' }, sess.answers.map(function (a) { return h('div', { class: 'mark-sq' + (a.correct ? '' : ' ng') }, [a.correct ? '✓' : '✕']); }))
    ]));
    const gateAnswers = sess.answers.filter(function (a) { return a.gate; });
    let gateText, gateColor;
    if (rematch) {
      const win = !r.gateFailed && r.correct >= Math.ceil(r.total * 0.7);
      gateText = win ? 'たおした！' : 'もう少し'; gateColor = win ? '#6cbd6f' : '#e2584c';
    } else if (!gateAnswers.length) { gateText = '関所は なし'; gateColor = '#fbf9f2'; }
    else if (!r.gateFailed) { gateText = gateAnswers.length + '／' + gateAnswers.length + ' 通った'; gateColor = '#6cbd6f'; }
    else { gateText = '落ちた　この階を やり直し'; gateColor = '#e2584c'; }
    const guardId = UI.guardOfSession(sess);
    const gs = MT.Sprites.fitSprite(guardId, 52, 44);
    left.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'gate-card' }, [gs ? h('div', { style: 'flex-shrink:0' }, [gs]) : null, h('div', { style: 'min-width:0' }, [h('div', { class: 'l', text: rematch ? '番人' : '関所' }), h('div', { class: 'v', style: 'color:' + gateColor, text: gateText })])]));
    const todo = h('div', { class: 'todo-card' }, [h('div', { class: 'l', text: r.wrong.length ? 'まちがい直しで やること' : 'まちがい なし' })]);
    // 多いときは 2列にして、ぜんぶ 見えるようにする
    const todoList = h('div', { class: 'todo-list' + (r.wrong.length > 6 ? ' two' : '') });
    r.wrong.forEach(function (a) {
      todoList.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'todo-row' }, [h('span', { class: 'n', plain: (a.index + 1) + '番' }), h('span', { class: 't', text: MT.Titles.title(a.templateId) }), h('span', { class: 'k', text: a.gate ? '関所' : MT.Titles.tagOf(a) })]));
    });
    if (r.wrong.length) todo.appendChild(todoList);
    left.appendChild(todo);

    // 右: 1問ずつ
    const right = h('div', { class: 'q-right' });
    body.appendChild(right);
    const list = h('div', { class: 'res-list' });
    sess.answers.forEach(function (a, i) {
      const tag = a.gate ? h('span', { class: 'tg gate', text: '関所' }) : (!a.correct ? h('span', { class: 'tg redo', text: 'やり直し' }) : null);
      list.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'res-row' + (a.correct ? '' : ' ng') }, [
        h('span', { class: 'n', plain: String(i + 1) }),
        h('span', { class: 'g' }, [a.correct ? '✓' : '✕']),
        h('span', { class: 't', text: MT.Titles.title(a.templateId) }),
        tag
      ]));
    });
    right.appendChild(list);

    const n = r.wrong.length;
    let primaryLabel, primary, secondaryLabel, secondary;
    if (rematch && dungeonRematch) {
      primaryLabel = 'ずかんへ　▶';
      primary = function () { E.endRematch(); st.view = 'zukan'; ctx.render(); };
      secondaryLabel = 'ホームへ';
      secondary = function () { E.endRematch(); st.view = null; ctx.render(); };
    } else if (rematch) {
      primaryLabel = n ? 'まちがい直し（' + n + '問）' : '次へ　▶';
      primary = function () { E.beginRetry(); ctx.render(); };
      secondaryLabel = 'ずかんへ';
      secondary = function () { E.endRematch(); st.view = 'zukan'; ctx.render(); };
    } else {
      primaryLabel = n ? 'まちがい直し（' + n + '問）' : '上の階へ';
      primary = function () { E.beginRetry(); ctx.render(); };
      secondaryLabel = 'ホームへ';
      secondary = function () { E.goMap(); ctx.render(); };
    }
    right.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'res-actions' }, [
      h('button', { class: 'big-btn', type: 'button', onclick: primary }, [UI.tx(primaryLabel)]),
      h('button', { class: 'big-btn navy', type: 'button', onclick: secondary }, [UI.tx(secondaryLabel)])
    ]));
    ctx.setKey(function (k) { if (k === 'enter') primary(); });
    app.appendChild(scr);
    UI.se(r.gateFailed ? 'gate' : (n ? 'tap' : 'clear'));
  };

  UI.screens.outcome = function (ctx) {
    const E = ctx.engine, st = ctx.state;
    const o = E.outcome();
    if (!o) { E.acknowledgeOutcome(); ctx.render(); return; }
    if (o.outcome === 'roof') { E.acknowledgeOutcome(); ctx.render(); return; }
    UI.bg('result', {}, { tint: 'rgba(31,39,64,0.3)' });
    const app = document.getElementById('app');
    const scr = h('div', { class: 'screen' });
    const box = h('div', { class: 'outcome' });
    scr.appendChild(box);
    const Story = MT.Story;
    let go1, go1Label, go2, go2Label;
    if (o.outcome === 'clear') {
      const lines = Story.clear(o.floorNo);
      box.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'outcome-title', text: o.floorNo + '階 クリア！' }));
      box.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'speaker-row' }, [UI.guardBox(MT.Guards.guardFor(o.floorNo)), h('div', { class: 'bubble', text: quoteOf(lines[0]) })]));
      if (lines[1]) box.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'outcome-note', text: lines[1] }));
      const nextNo = o.floorNo + 1;
      if (nextNo <= 14) {
        const enter = Story.enter(nextNo);
        box.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'speaker-row' }, [UI.guardBox(MT.Guards.guardFor(nextNo)), h('div', { class: 'bubble long' }, [h('span', { class: 'step-ask', style: 'margin:0', text: '次は ' + nextNo + '階' }), UI.tx(quoteOf(enter[0]))])]));
      }
      go1Label = '次の階へ　▶'; go1 = function () { E.acknowledgeOutcome(); E.startFloor(); ctx.render(); };
      go2Label = 'ホームへ'; go2 = function () { E.acknowledgeOutcome(); ctx.render(); };
      UI.se('clear');
    } else if (o.outcome === 'redo') {
      box.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'outcome-title redo', text: '番人に 止められた' }));
      box.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'speaker-row' }, [UI.guardBox(MT.Guards.guardFor(o.floorNo)), h('div', { class: 'bubble', text: quoteOf(Story.FLOOR_REDO[0]) })]));
      box.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'outcome-note', text: Story.FLOOR_REDO[1] }));
      go1Label = 'もう一度 ちょうせん　▶'; go1 = function () { E.acknowledgeOutcome(); E.startFloor(); ctx.render(); };
      go2Label = 'ホームへ'; go2 = function () { E.acknowledgeOutcome(); ctx.render(); };
      UI.se('gate');
    } else {
      const sp = MT.Sprites.byId(o.guardId);
      box.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'outcome-title' + (o.win ? '' : ' redo'), text: o.win ? 'たおした！' : 'まだまだ…' }));
      box.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'speaker-row' }, [UI.guardBox(o.guardId), h('div', { class: 'bubble', text: o.win ? '見事だ。また いつでも 来い。' : 'まだまだ だな。もう一度 来い。' })]));
      box.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'outcome-note', text: (sp ? sp.name + '　' : '') + 'せいかい ' + o.correct + '／' + o.total }));
      go1Label = 'もう一度 戦う'; go1 = function () { E.acknowledgeOutcome(); const res = E.startRematch(o.guardId); if (!res) st.view = 'zukan'; ctx.render(); };
      go2Label = 'ずかんへ'; go2 = function () { E.acknowledgeOutcome(); st.view = 'zukan'; ctx.render(); };
      UI.se(o.win ? 'fanfare' : 'gate');
    }
    box.appendChild(h('div', { class: 'outcome-actions' }, [
      h('button', { class: 'big-btn', type: 'button', onclick: go1 }, [UI.tx(go1Label)]),
      h('button', { class: 'big-btn navy', type: 'button', onclick: go2 }, [UI.tx(go2Label)])
    ]));
    ctx.setKey(function (k) { if (k === 'enter') go1(); });
    app.appendChild(scr);
  };
})(typeof globalThis !== 'undefined' ? globalThis : this);


/* 面積の塔: 導入（8階・9階の前）・屋上・地下クリア・エンディング・番人ずかん */
(function (root) {
  'use strict';
  const MT = root.MT;
  const UI = MT.UI;
  const h = UI.h;
